```python
# --- Plans and blocks (engine spec §13.1, §13.3, §13.5, §13.8; engine build C2b) --------------
#
# **A task's plans live in its task object** (the PI, 2026-10-07: "In the task file": "beside its
# trial(s), one reviewable file; the operator chooses at run start among the plans it declares").
# A plan is its block types, how they follow one another, and a run time limit. A block type is a
# named set of conditions; **a block is one stretch of consecutive trials under one block type
# inside one run** (the PI, 2026-10-01: "it is important that blocks are temporal instances"):
# types recur, and each occurrence is a new block. The vocabulary lives here, the task's, so the
# content hash and its format pins read it as they read every declaration; `scheduler` plays it
# and imports it from here.


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Order:
    """How the next condition is chosen. **Declared per block** (PI, 2026-08-31)."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Shuffled(Order):
    """Every still-owed condition once per pass, in random order.

    Balance as the block progresses, and a bound on how long an animal waits for any
    condition. Partly predictable near the end of a pass, when few remain.
    """


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class WithReplacement(Order):
    """Each trial drawn independently, optionally weighted.

    No sequential predictability, and it supports conditions wanted rarely -- catch
    trials, probes. Balance only in expectation, so the counters matter more.
    """

    weights: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Constrained(Order):
    """Drawn with replacement, refusing runs longer than `max_run`.

    What most search and attention designs actually use, because an animal exploits
    runs: three of the same target position in a row and it stops searching and
    starts predicting.
    """

    max_run: int = 2
    weights: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Condition:
    """One condition of a block (engine spec §14.1): its `name`, the `values` it sets, the trials
    it is owed (`target`; a minimum where its block has another end rule), and **the trial
    structure it plays** (`structure`, one of the task's by name; `None`, the task's default) --
    the PI, 2026-10-07: "a task declares several trial structures; each condition names its own;
    interleaving them in a block is ordinary". Its trials count by that structure's table
    (`Trial.counting`) unless its block counts by one of its own. **A value it sets gives way to
    a live edit** of the same setting for the rest of the run (engine spec §14.7), and the trial
    still counts toward it."""

    name: str
    values: dict
    target: int
    structure: "str | None" = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Block:
    """One block type (engine spec §13.5): its `name`, its `conditions`, and how a block of it
    ends -- every condition's target used up, and where it declares them a criterion, a number of
    trials, or a time limit -- with how it orders its conditions, what it counts by and whether it
    drums. A name is what each trial's row records as its `block`, the block's type (wl-preproc
    reads it so), so no two block types of one plan share one."""

    name: str
    conditions: "list[Condition]"
    #: `(proportion, window)` -- "80% correct over the last 20 answers", a percentage; a proportion
    #: of 1.0 is a streak, "N correct in a row" (the engine C2a plan, revised 2026-10-18). Judged on
    #: every answer, and on a broken hold or a failure to acquire fixation where this block's own
    #: table scores it (`scheduler.Scheduler._scored`); a percentage judges only each trial's first
    #: answer under `first_answers`, a streak never. The block ends once every condition is owed
    #: nothing and, with a criterion, the criterion is met too: **its targets are a minimum**
    #: (`scheduler.Scheduler.finished`).
    criterion: "tuple[float, int] | None" = None
    #: The table this block counts by, **replacing its conditions' structures'** (TSK-26: what
    #: counts is declared per block): `scheduler.CORRECT_ONLY`, `PRESENTED`, `EVERY_TRIAL` or any
    #: `Counting` -- a block training an animal to hold fixation may count a fixation break as an
    #: error that pays its debt -- and its rule then judges that break a miss (the PI, 2026-10-18,
    #: "Yes, where the block says so"; `scheduler.Scheduler._scored`) -- while a block measuring a
    #: psychometric function must not. What is an answer stays the structure's: a block's own
    #: `withholding` is not read, nor its `appears` (`scheduler.Scheduler._class`;
    #: `taskd._appeared`). `None`, each condition's structure's own.
    counting: "Counting | None" = None
    order: Order = field(default_factory=Shuffled)
    #: **Drumming** (the PI, 2026-10-11: "a training tool ... where a trial is repeated until
    #: correct"): every trial of this block that is not right comes back at once, paying nothing,
    #: until it is. A trainer switches it on or off for a block or a run from the console as well
    #: (`scheduler.Scheduler.drum`).
    drum: bool = False
    #: **A number of trials** (engine spec §13.5; the PI, 2026-10-07, "a number of trials"): the
    #: block ends once it has run this many trials -- a trial and its drummed repeats one (the PI,
    #: 2026-10-18: "200 trials; drummed repeats inside them"), an ordinary repeat a trial of its own
    #: -- **and every target is used up**: its targets are a minimum, as under a criterion, and past
    #: them every condition is drawn by its order (`scheduler.Scheduler.past_targets`). `None`, no
    #: such rule.
    trials: "int | None" = None
    #: **A time limit**, in seconds of trials run (engine spec §13.5; the PI, 2026-10-19): read
    #: between trials, so a trial in flight finishes and pays if earned ("Finish trial, then end");
    #: a drummed trial waiting comes back until it is right first ("Finish the drum first"); time
    #: paused does not count ("Pauses don't count"). What the block still owes then is recorded as
    #: not run, "time ran out" (`scheduler.TIME`). `None`, no limit. Not a welfare limit: the
    #: out-of-cage limit ends the session whatever a block says.
    time_limit_s: "float | None" = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BlockOrder:
    """How a plan's blocks follow one another (engine spec §13.5; the PI, 2026-10-07: "a fixed
    sequence; repeating (Bt1, Bt2, Bt1, Bt2...); randomized from a recorded seed, balanced;
    progression by criterion (training stages, shaping)"). Declared per plan;
    `scheduler.Scheduler` plays it, drawing a random order from the run's recorded seed."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Fixed(BlockOrder):
    """Each block type once, in the order the plan lists them; the plan is done after the last."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Cyclic(BlockOrder):
    """The plan's block types in order, over and over (Bt1, Bt2, Bt1, Bt2...): `cycles` passes, or
    with `None` until a time limit, a stop or the out-of-cage limit ends the run."""

    cycles: "int | None" = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Balanced(BlockOrder):
    """**Shuffled rounds, within each run** (the PI, 2026-10-11: "A 'balanced' block order balances
    within each run"; 2026-10-19, "Shuffled rounds"): each round shows every block type once, in an
    order drawn from the run's seed, **and never the type just played first** ("Never
    back-to-back": "Each new round starts with a different type than the last one played, so every
    block boundary is a real switch"); `blocks` in all, or with `None` round after round until a
    time limit, a stop or the out-of-cage limit. A count that is not whole rounds leaves the last
    round partial, the seed choosing which types it shows (a warning at load); a run cut short
    leaves its last round incomplete, as recorded."""

    blocks: "int | None" = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Random(BlockOrder):
    """**Each block a type drawn at random** from the run's seed (the PI, 2026-10-19, "Both, as two
    choices": "random can repeat a type and leave another out by chance, recorded with its seed"):
    `blocks` in all, or with `None` until a time limit, a stop or the out-of-cage limit."""

    blocks: "int | None" = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ByCriterion(BlockOrder):
    """**A training progression** (the PI, 2026-10-07: "advance on one criterion, fall back on
    another, as declared"; 2026-10-19, "Same, but top keeps going" and "Move on anyway"): the
    plan's block types are its stages, easiest first, and the run starts at the first. **A stage
    whose own rule is met moves up one**; a stage whose time runs out moves up one whether it
    passed or not. **`fall_back`, `(proportion, window)`**: back one stage, started fresh, when
    fewer than `proportion` of the last `window` answers are right, judged once that many answers
    are in and on answers only, as every rule -- **each trial's first answer only**, however the
    stage's own rule counts drummed tries ("First answer only": "so one stubborn trial can't demote
    a stage the animal is otherwise doing well in"), and a broken hold where the stage's own table
    scores one, as toward moving up ("Yes, the same"). **At the top stage the animal keeps
    working** once its rule is met, until a time limit, a stop or the session's end; the top
    stage's own time limit, with nowhere to move on to, ends the run. `None`, no fall-back."""

    fall_back: "tuple[float, int] | None" = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Plan:
    """One plan (engine spec §13.1): its block types (`blocks`), how they follow one another
    (`order`, `Fixed()` by default), and **a run time limit** (`time_limit_s`, in seconds of trials
    run; the PI, 2026-10-19, "Yes, in the plan": "the run ends at the first pause between trials
    after its time", pauses not counting, a drummed trial waiting answered right first, "Finish
    the drum first") -- which is no welfare limit: the out-of-cage limit ends the session whatever
    a plan says, and the console's scheduled stop stays as it is, whichever comes first ending the
    run. **Its shape is refused as it is built**, as a `Task`'s is; what it declares is checked at
    load (`check.check_task`)."""

    blocks: "list[Block]"
    order: BlockOrder = field(default_factory=Fixed)
    time_limit_s: "float | None" = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.blocks, list) or not all(
                isinstance(block, Block) for block in self.blocks):
            raise ValueError("a plan's blocks are a list of Blocks")
        if not isinstance(self.order, BlockOrder):
            raise ValueError(
                "a plan's order is a BlockOrder: Fixed(), Cyclic(), Balanced(), Random() or "
                "ByCriterion()"
            )
        if self.time_limit_s is not None and (
                isinstance(self.time_limit_s, bool)
                or not isinstance(self.time_limit_s, (int, float))):
            raise ValueError(
                f"a plan's time limit is a number of seconds, or None; not {self.time_limit_s!r}"
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Task:
    """One task (engine spec §3.5): its trial structures by name (§13.3), `default` the one the
    plain run plays and a condition naming no structure plays, its plans by name (§13.1; engine
    build C2b), and what XC-288 §4 has it declare: what its settings mean beyond the shared list
    (`meanings`; `declarations.COMMON`), what it offers to choose -- a `Choice`, or `NO_CHOICE` --
    how its reaction time is defined, and whether it needs a fixed head. **Its shape is refused as
```

### Task 4: Each condition plays its own structure, and a live edit wins over its value

The PI's decision 22 (2026-10-07): "a task declares several trial structures; each condition names its own; interleaving them in a block is ordinary." **The loop builds and runs each trial from its condition's structure** -- the one `Condition.structure` names, the task's default where it names none (call 17) -- where it played the default for every trial (`taskd.py:2396`, "until build C2's plans name others"), and **the scheduler counts each condition by that structure's table** (`Scheduler.countings`), where one run-level table answered for every condition in five methods (`_table`, `_cap`, `_answer`, `_class`, `clearable`; Surprise 11): what a break after the stimulus does, whether no response is an answer, the repeat limit. A block's own table still replaces them all, what is an answer staying each structure's (the controller's ruling M8 on C2a). **And a live edit wins over a value a condition sets** (engine spec §14.7; TSK-19; call 27): the loop put a condition's values over every live edit (`{**self.spec.values, **condition.values}`, Surprise 9), the reverse of the spec, harmless while the only condition set none; from the boundary a setting is applied live (`_apply_staged`) it wins for the rest of the run -- setting it back does not undo that -- the trial still counting toward its condition, and its row lists the value overridden (`overridden`, §14.7's flag). **Until C3's console shows which conditions an edit overrides, the edit's line on the changes feed names them** (`_overriding`; the science review, I5). Each trial's row names its `structure`. The simulator keeps playing the default (call 30).

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/scheduler.py`, `wl_xcon/taskd.py`, `wl_xcon/record.py`, `wl_xcon/simulate.py`
- Modify (docs): the engine spec (§13.3's and §14.7's notes), `docs/references/decisions.md` (TSK-19)
- Modify (test): `tests/_sessions.py` (`planned_task`), `tests/test_scheduler.py`, `tests/test_taskd.py`, `tests/test_simulate.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `task.Condition.structure` (Task 3).
- Produces: `Scheduler(..., countings: dict[str, Counting] = {})`, `_structure(condition)`, `_table(condition)`, `_cap(condition)`, `_answer(outcome, condition)`, `_class(outcome, shown, condition)`; `Session._edited: frozenset`; `taskd._overriding(blocks, name) -> str`; `SessionRecord.trial(..., structure=None, overridden=())`, each line's `structure` and `overridden`; `tests/_sessions.planned_task(folder, plans="{}", *, name="planned", structures="TASK.structures") -> Path`, the reference detection task with plans, engine test material.

```python
def planned_task(folder: Path, plans: str = "{}", *, name: str = "planned",
                 structures: str = "TASK.structures") -> Path:
    """The reference detection task with plans (engine build C2b), written into `folder` as
    `NAME.py`: `plans` is Python source for its plans dict, and `structures` for its structures
    dict, each free to name `detection`, its one trial structure, and every word of the plan
    vocabulary. Engine test material, run only in the simulator: no reference task declares a
    plan, since real plans are set with the PI in wl-xtasks."""
    text = Path("tasks/fixation_detection.py").read_text() + (
        "\n\nimport dataclasses as _dataclasses\n"
        "from wl_xcon.task import (\n"
        "    NO_LIMIT, SPENT, Balanced, Block, ByCriterion, Condition, Constrained, Cyclic,\n"
        "    Fixed, Plan, Random, WithReplacement,\n"
        ")\n"
        f"TASK = _dataclasses.replace(TASK, structures={structures}, plans={plans})\n"
    )
    path = Path(folder) / f"{name}.py"
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(text)
    return path


```

```python
def _mixed(**block) -> Scheduler:
    """A block of two conditions, `detect` naming the `detection` structure -- breaks after its
    stimulus used up, no response a miss, no repeat -- and `search` naming none, so playing the
    default's: breaks shown again, no response no answer, no limit (engine build C2b)."""
    detection = Counting(after_stimulus=SPENT, withholding=True, cap=0)
    search = Counting(after_stimulus=OWED)
    mixed = Block(name="mixed", conditions=[
        Condition("detect", {}, target=5, structure="detection"),
        Condition("search", {}, target=5),
    ], **block)
    return Scheduler([mixed], seed=1, counting=search, countings={"detection": detection})


def test_each_condition_counts_by_the_table_of_the_structure_it_plays():
    """Engine build C2b (the PI, 2026-10-07: "a task declares several trial structures; each
    condition names its own; interleaving them in a block is ordinary"): a condition naming a
    structure counts by that structure's table -- what a break after its stimulus does, whether no
    response is an answer, its repeat limit -- and one naming none by the default's."""
    scheduler = _mixed()

    assert scheduler.record("detect", Outcome.TARGET_BREAK) == SPENT
    assert scheduler.record("search", Outcome.TARGET_BREAK) == OWED
    assert scheduler.record("detect", Outcome.NO_RESPONSE) == COUNTED
    assert scheduler.record("search", Outcome.NO_RESPONSE) == OWED
    # `detect`'s structure moves a condition past at its first failure in a row; `search`'s has
    # no limit, so its failures were placed as repeats.
    assert scheduler.record("detect", Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK) == OWED
    assert [(s.condition, s.because) for s in scheduler.shortfalls] == [("detect", CAP)]
    assert scheduler.counts("search").repeats == 2
    # And judged by it: `detect`'s miss is a wrong answer, `search`'s no response no answer.
    assert (scheduler.counts("detect").wrong, scheduler.counts("search").wrong) == (1, 0)


def test_a_blocks_own_table_replaces_each_structures_and_each_structure_says_what_is_an_answer():
    """A block counting by its own table counts every condition by it, whatever structure each
    plays; whether no response is an answer stays each structure's (the controller's ruling M8 on
    the engine C2a plan's revision): a miss under `detect`, used up as no answer under `search`."""
    scheduler = _mixed(counting=PRESENTED)

    assert scheduler.record("detect", Outcome.TARGET_BREAK) == SPENT
    assert scheduler.record("search", Outcome.TARGET_BREAK) == SPENT
    assert scheduler.record("detect", Outcome.NO_RESPONSE) == COUNTED
    assert scheduler.record("search", Outcome.NO_RESPONSE) == SPENT


def test_a_block_offers_clear_when_any_structure_its_conditions_play_can_owe():
    """`clearable` (the engine C2a plan's Minor 3) asks every structure the open block's conditions
    play: a block of conditions all playing a structure that owes nothing offers no *clear*; one
    more condition playing a structure that owes, and it does."""
    def offered(*structures) -> bool:
        block = Block(name="b", conditions=[
            Condition(f"c{n}", {}, target=1, structure=name) for n, name in enumerate(structures)])
        return Scheduler([block], seed=1, counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED),
                         countings={"flat": EVERY_TRIAL}).clearable

    assert offered("flat", "flat") is False
    assert offered("flat", None) is True


```

```python
def test_each_condition_plays_the_structure_it_names_and_its_row_says_which(tmp_path):
    """Engine spec §13.3 (the PI, 2026-10-07: "each condition names its own; interleaving them in a
    block is ordinary"; engine build C2b): each trial is built and run from its condition's
    structure, the task's default for one naming none, and its row names the structure played."""
    path = _sessions.planned_task(tmp_path, structures=(
        '{"detection": detection, "spent": _dataclasses.replace(detection, counting='
        '_dataclasses.replace(detection.counting, after_stimulus=SPENT))}'))
    task = _load_task(path)
    block = Block(name="mixed", conditions=[Condition("plain", {}, target=3),
                                            Condition("used", {}, target=3, structure="spent")])
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, task=str(path), trials=400, blocks=[block])
    subject = Subject(seed=1, hazards=spec.hazards, engagement=spec.engagement, lapse=spec.lapse)
    played = []

    def world(trial, values, index):
        played.append(trial)
        subject.new_trial()
        prepare(subject, trial, spec.frame_period, values)
        return subject

    session = _session(spec)
    session.world = world

    session.run()

    rows = _trial_rows(session)
    assert {(row["condition"], row["structure"]) for row in rows} == {
        ("plain", "detection"), ("used", "spent")}
    assert [trial.counting for trial in played] == [
        task.structures[row["structure"]].counting for row in rows]


def test_a_live_edit_wins_over_a_conditions_value_and_its_rows_say_so(tmp_path):
    """Engine spec §14.7 (§17.1's order: the task's start, the run's value, the condition's, a live
    edit): "A live edit to a value a condition sets wins, and the trial still counts toward its
    condition", the trial table carrying an "overridden" flag. A value the condition does not set
    is overridden by nothing; before the edit the condition's value holds."""
    link = _Drained({3: [SetParameter(name="target_x", value=12.0, by=Box("jake"))]})
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, trials=400, blocks=[
        Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("near", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=6)])])
    session = _session(spec, link=link)

    session.run()

    rows = _trial_rows(session)
    assert [(row["params"]["target_x"], row["overridden"]) for row in rows[:3]] == [
        (5.0, [])] * 3
    assert {(row["params"]["target_x"], tuple(row["overridden"])) for row in rows[3:]} == {
        (12.0, ("target_x",))}
    assert {row["condition"] for row in rows} == {"near"}
    assert session.stopped_because == "every block is finished"


def test_a_live_edits_feed_line_names_the_conditions_it_now_overrides(tmp_path):
    """The engine C2b plan's science review, I5: until the console shows which conditions an edit
    overrides (build C3), the edit's line on the changes feed names them -- every condition of the
    run's blocks that sets the value -- and an edit of a value no condition sets names none."""
    link = _Drained({1: [SetParameter(name="target_x", value=12.0, by=Box("jake")),
                         SetParameter(name="target_y", value=2.0, by=Box("jake"))]})
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, trials=400, blocks=[
        Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("near", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=2),
                                    Condition("far", {"target_x": 9.0}, target=2)])])
    session = _session(spec, link=link)

    session.run()

    assert [said for kind, _, _, said in session.controls if kind == "set"] == [
        "target_x 10.00 → 12.00, from trial 1; for the rest of the run it overrides the value "
        "conditions near, far set",
        "target_y 0.00 → 2.00, from trial 1",
    ]

```

`wl_xcon/scheduler.py`: replace

```python
    @property
    def _table(self) -> Counting | None:
        """The open block's counting table, else the run's. (`_class` still asks the run's whether
        no response is an answer: what counts as one is the structure's.)"""
        return self.block.counting if self.block.counting is not None else self.counting

    @property
    def _cap(self) -> int | None:
        """The open block's repeat limit: its own table's, **else the structure's** -- a block that
        counts by a table of its own keeps the task's limit unless its table sets one (the PI,
        2026-10-18: "No: the task's limit applies unless the block sets its own") -- a number, or
        `task.NO_LIMIT`, which removes it (XC-380). `None`, no limit."""
        own = self.block.counting
        if own is not None and own.cap is not None:
            return None if own.cap == NO_LIMIT else own.cap
        if self.counting is None or self.counting.cap == NO_LIMIT:
            return None
        return self.counting.cap

    def _answer(self, outcome: Outcome | None) -> bool:
        """Whether `outcome` is an answer, right or wrong (the PI, 2026-10-18): one of
        `task.ANSWERS` -- a correct answer, a correct rejection, a wrong target, a false alarm, an
        early or a late response or error -- or no response where the structure makes withholding
        an answer, a miss (`Counting.withholding`, the structure's, as `_class` reads it). A trial
        with no answer -- a break, no fixation, a lost tracker, a fault, a hang -- is none."""
        if outcome in ANSWERS:
            return True
        return (outcome is Outcome.NO_RESPONSE and self.counting is not None
                and self.counting.withholding)
```

with

```python
    def _structure(self, condition: str) -> Counting | None:
        """The counting table of the structure `condition` plays (engine build C2b): the one it
        names (`task.Condition.structure`, read in `countings`), else the default's (`counting`).
        What counts as an answer, and the repeat limit where its block sets none, are its."""
        named = next(c for c in self.block.conditions if c.name == condition).structure
        if named is None:
            return self.counting
        return self.countings.get(named, self.counting)

    def _table(self, condition: str) -> Counting | None:
        """The table `condition`'s trials count by: its block's own, else its structure's.
        (`_class` still asks the structure's whether no response is an answer: what counts as one
        is the structure's.)"""
        own = self.block.counting
        return own if own is not None else self._structure(condition)

    def _cap(self, condition: str) -> int | None:
        """`condition`'s repeat limit in the open block: its block's own table's, **else its
        structure's** -- a block that counts by a table of its own keeps the task's limit unless its
        table sets one (the PI, 2026-10-18: "No: the task's limit applies unless the block sets its
        own") -- a number, or `task.NO_LIMIT`, which removes it (XC-380). `None`, no limit."""
        own = self.block.counting
        if own is not None and own.cap is not None:
            return None if own.cap == NO_LIMIT else own.cap
        structure = self._structure(condition)
        if structure is None or structure.cap == NO_LIMIT:
            return None
        return structure.cap

    def _answer(self, outcome: Outcome | None, condition: str) -> bool:
        """Whether `outcome` is an answer, right or wrong (the PI, 2026-10-18): one of
        `task.ANSWERS` -- a correct answer, a correct rejection, a wrong target, a false alarm, an
        early or a late response or error -- or no response where `condition`'s structure makes
        withholding an answer, a miss (`Counting.withholding`, the structure's, as `_class` reads
        it). A trial with no answer -- a break, no fixation, a lost tracker, a fault, a hang -- is
        none."""
        if outcome in ANSWERS:
            return True
        structure = self._structure(condition)
        return (outcome is Outcome.NO_RESPONSE and structure is not None
                and structure.withholding)
```

```python

def _overriding(blocks, name: str) -> str:
    """What a live edit of `name` now overrides, for its line on the changes feed (engine spec
    §14.7; the engine C2b plan's science review, I5): the conditions of the run's blocks that set
    it, whose value the edit replaces for the rest of the run -- setting it back does not undo
    that -- since the console's own showing of them is build C3's. Empty when none sets it."""
    named = list(dict.fromkeys(
        condition.name for block in blocks for condition in block.conditions
        if name in condition.values))
    if not named:
        return ""
    return (f"; for the rest of the run it overrides the value condition"
            f"{'s' if len(named) > 1 else ''} {', '.join(named)} set")

```

- [ ] **Step 6: Prove they can fail**

- `wl_xcon/scheduler.py`: `return self.countings.get(named, self.counting)` → `return self.counting` -- `tests/test_scheduler.py::test_each_condition_counts_by_the_table_of_the_structure_it_plays` fails: `1 failed`.
- `wl_xcon/scheduler.py`: `structure = self._structure(condition)` → `structure = self.counting` -- `tests/test_scheduler.py::test_each_condition_counts_by_the_table_of_the_structure_it_plays` fails: `1 failed`.
- `wl_xcon/taskd.py`: `structure = condition.structure or task.default` → `structure = task.default` -- `tests/test_taskd.py::test_each_condition_plays_the_structure_it_names_and_its_row_says_which` fails: `1 failed`.
- `wl_xcon/taskd.py`: `**{name: value for name, value in condition.values.items() if name not in self._edited},` → `**condition.values,` -- `tests/test_taskd.py::test_a_live_edit_wins_over_a_conditions_value_and_its_rows_say_so` fails: `1 failed`.
- `wl_xcon/taskd.py`: `+ ("" if bounded else _overriding(self._scheduler.blocks, name)),` → `+ "",` -- `tests/test_taskd.py::test_a_live_edits_feed_line_names_the_conditions_it_now_overrides` fails: `1 failed`.

Each applied alone, its test run alone (`$PY -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider <test>`), and the file restored with `git checkout -- <file>`.

### Task 5: The scheduler plays a plan's order, and knows why each block ends

Where a block-sequence policy plugs in (code map §2): the sequence was a list walked by `advance` (`_index += 1`, raising past the last), `done` (`_index >= len(blocks) - 1`) and the drum carry's `self.blocks[self._index + 1]` -- three places a cyclic, random or falling-back plan breaks (Surprise 4). **The scheduler now keeps the open block's place in the run's sequence** and reads its type from the plan's order (`_type_at`, call 5): **`Fixed`** in order once; **`Cyclic`** over and over; **`Balanced`** in shuffled rounds within the run, the last round partial (Q8), **never one type back-to-back where rounds meet** -- a round that would start with the type just played turned by one (answer 16; the science review, M1); **`Random`** each block drawn (Q8b) -- both random orders from a generator of their own, seeded from the run's seed, so they replay and every condition's draw is as the seed gives it; **`ByCriterion`** stage by stage. **`ends`** says why the open block ends now: **its own rule** (`RULE`: targets, and as declared a criterion and **a number of trials** -- a trial and its drummed repeats one, decision 31 -- its targets a minimum, call 4) -- **but a training progression's top stage keeps working** (Q3), its conditions drawn on (`past_targets`); **the plan's fall-back rule** (`FALL_BACK`: fewer than its proportion right of its last window of answers, judged once that many are in, never at the first stage, never while the stage's own rule holds, call 28) -- **its window apart from the stage rule's, each trial's first answer only, a broken hold the stage scores included** (answers 13 and 14; the science review, C1: the window sat in the stage rule's branch, so it took every drummed try and followed the "first answers only" switch only under a percentage); **its time** (`TIME`, once `expire` is called -- by the loop, Task 7) -- **never while a drummed trial waits** (Q2). `advance(reason, by)` moves on one, or back one for a fall-back (started fresh, Q3), a timed-out stage moving on whether or not it passed (Q4); `close` records how each block ended (`BlockEnd`, with **its `position` in the run's sequence and its `block_in_session`**, the stream number the loop gives as its first trial opens it, `None` for a block that ran none -- the engineering review, I4 -- and a top stage's **`passed_at`**, the trial after which its rule was first met, the science review, I1), and what a timed-out block still owed as not run (`Shortfall` `TIME`, or **`RUN_TIME`** for the run's own limit, the science review, I3; forgiving nothing, Q1; call 9). `move` is a person's *next block* or *back one* (Q10; call 26), and **a move starts the progress check afresh** (the engineering review, I1, its fuzz's seed 167). `because` says a block end in words with its numbers, for the console's feed (the science review, I2), `fell_back` counts the fall-backs and `drum_waiting` names a drummed trial waiting, which the loop's run limit and a person's move read. **A condition of weight 0 is never drawn** (`_refill`), so a block whose every drawable condition weighs 0 has nothing to draw, where `random.choices` raised (the engineering review, I2). The drum carry reads the block that follows (`following`). **The progress check's two questions** (Q7; call 7): `stalled`, every block type opened since the last trial and each ended before it drew one; `drawable`, whether the open block can draw. Nothing here reads a clock (`scheduler.py:1-14`). A fuzz of 400 random plans pins the invariants, balanced types never back to back among them. The docs: TSK-57 (answers 13 and 14; its citation of `berger2018standardized` held to what decisions.md records, the step back UNVERIFIED -- the science review, M6) and TSK-58 (answer 16). Files **XC-405** (a progression started at a later stage, "Not in C2b"), "Next free" raised to XC-406.

```python
# --- Plans: how blocks follow one another, and how each ends (engine build C2b) ---------------


def _one(name: str, target: int = 1, **block) -> Block:
    return Block(name=name, conditions=[Condition(name.lower(), {}, target=target)], **block)


def _run_out(scheduler: Scheduler, limit: int = 60) -> list[str]:
    """The block names a scheduler visits, every trial correct, until its plan is done."""
    seen = [scheduler.block.name]
    for _ in range(limit):
        if scheduler.done:
            return seen
        if scheduler.ends is not None:
            seen.append(scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends).name)
            continue
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)
    raise AssertionError(f"not done after {limit} steps: {seen}")


def test_a_cyclic_plan_repeats_its_blocks_in_order_its_cycles_times():
    """The PI, 2026-10-07: "repeating (Bt1, Bt2, Bt1, Bt2...)". A cyclic plan of no count is never
    done by its blocks; a time limit, a stop or the out-of-cage limit ends its run."""
    blocks = [_one("A"), _one("B")]

    assert _run_out(Scheduler(blocks, seed=1, order=Cyclic(cycles=2))) == ["A", "B", "A", "B"]
    endless = Scheduler(blocks, seed=1, order=Cyclic())
    for _ in range(9):
        endless.record(endless.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)
        assert not endless.done
        endless.advance(endless.ends)
    assert endless.block.name == "B"


def test_a_balanced_plan_shows_every_block_type_once_a_round_in_an_order_drawn_from_the_seed():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Shuffled rounds": each round shows every block type once, in a seeded
    random order; a count that is not whole rounds leaves the last round partial, the seed choosing
    its types; the same seed replays the same order, and another seed can draw another."""
    blocks = [_one("A"), _one("B"), _one("C")]

    def order(seed: int, count: int = 7) -> list[str]:
        return _run_out(Scheduler(blocks, seed=seed, order=Balanced(blocks=count)))

    first = order(1)
    assert len(first) == 7
    assert sorted(first[0:3]) == sorted(first[3:6]) == ["A", "B", "C"]
    assert first == order(1)
    assert len({tuple(order(seed)) for seed in range(8)}) > 1


def test_a_random_plan_draws_each_block_type_from_the_seed_and_may_repeat_one():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Both, as two choices": "random can repeat a type and leave another out
    by chance, recorded with its seed": each block's type drawn on its own, replayed by the seed."""
    blocks = [_one("A"), _one("B")]
    orders = [tuple(_run_out(Scheduler(blocks, seed=seed, order=Random(blocks=6))))
              for seed in range(12)]

    assert all(len(order) == 6 for order in orders)
    assert any(order[i] == order[i + 1] for order in orders for i in range(5))
    assert orders[3] == tuple(_run_out(Scheduler(blocks, seed=3, order=Random(blocks=6))))


def test_a_random_block_order_leaves_every_conditions_draw_as_the_seed_gives_it():
    """A random block order draws from a generator of its own (`seed` again, apart), so the
    conditions a block draws are the ones a fixed plan's same block draws from the same seed."""
    block = Block(name="A", conditions=[Condition(c, {}, target=3) for c in "xyz"])

    def draws(**order) -> list[str]:
        scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=5, **order)
        names = []
        for _ in range(9):
            names.append(scheduler.next_trial().name)
            scheduler.record(names[-1], Outcome.CORRECT)
        return names

    assert draws() == draws(order=Random(blocks=1)) == draws(order=Balanced(blocks=1))


def _stages(fall_back=(0.5, 4)) -> Scheduler:
    """A training progression of three stages, each "3 right in a row" over one condition."""
    blocks = [_one(name, target=1, criterion=(1.0, 3)) for name in ("S1", "S2", "S3")]
    return Scheduler(blocks, seed=1, order=ByCriterion(fall_back=fall_back))


def _answer(scheduler: Scheduler, *outcomes: Outcome) -> None:
    for outcome in outcomes:
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, outcome)


def test_a_training_progression_moves_up_on_a_stages_rule_and_back_one_on_its_fall_back():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Same, but top keeps going": move up when the stage's own rule is met;
    move back one stage, started fresh, when the plan's second rule is met -- "under 50% over the
    last 20" -- judged once that many answers are in, answers only."""
    scheduler = _stages()
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.ends == RULE
    assert scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends).name == "S2"

    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET,
            Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.ends is None, "three answers are not yet the window's four"
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)
    assert scheduler.ends == FALL_BACK
    assert scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends).name == "S1"
    assert scheduler.counts("s1").trials == 0, "started fresh"
    assert scheduler.fell_back == 1, "counted, for the console's plan row"
    assert [(end.block, end.ended) for end in scheduler.ended] == [("S1", RULE), ("S2", FALL_BACK)]


def test_the_first_stage_has_no_stage_to_fall_back_to():
    scheduler = _stages()
    _answer(scheduler, *[Outcome.WRONG_TARGET] * 6)

    assert scheduler.ends is None


def test_at_the_top_stage_the_animal_keeps_working_once_its_rule_is_met():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19: "at the top stage the animal keeps working there until a time limit, a
    stop or the end of the session": its rule met, it does not end, and its conditions keep coming;
    its own time limit ends the run, with nowhere to move on to."""
    scheduler = _stages(fall_back=None)
    for _ in range(2):
        _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT)
        scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends)
    _answer(scheduler, *[Outcome.CORRECT] * 5)

    assert scheduler.block.name == "S3" and scheduler.finished
    assert scheduler.ends is None and not scheduler.done and scheduler.past_targets
    scheduler.expire()
    assert scheduler.ends == TIME and scheduler.done


def test_a_met_rule_counts_before_a_fall_back_at_the_top_stage_too():
    """A met rule counts before a fall-back met at the same answer ("training ends on a success";
    Appendix B) -- and at the top stage, which works on once its rule is met, it holds the
    fall-back off for as long as the rule holds, falling back once it does not."""
    blocks = [_one("S1", target=1), _one("S2", target=1, criterion=(1.0, 2))]
    scheduler = Scheduler(blocks, seed=1, order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.6, 4)))
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT)
    scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends)
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, Outcome.CORRECT,
            Outcome.CORRECT)

    assert scheduler.finished and list(scheduler._back) == [0, 0, 1, 1]
    assert scheduler.ends is None, "two right in a row hold off two of four first answers"
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)
    assert scheduler.ends == FALL_BACK


def test_a_stage_whose_time_runs_out_moves_up_whether_or_not_it_passed():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Move on anyway": "Time-boxed stages: go to the next stage whether or not
    the animal passed." What it still owed is recorded as not run, "time ran out"."""
    scheduler = _stages()
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK)
    scheduler.expire()

    assert scheduler.ends == TIME
    assert scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends).name == "S2"
    assert scheduler.shortfalls == [Shortfall("S1", "s1", 1, TIME, 0)]
    assert scheduler.ended == [BlockEnd("S1", TIME, None, 0)]


def test_a_blocks_time_waits_for_a_drummed_trial_to_be_right():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Finish the drum first": time running out while a drummed trial is coming
    back ends the block only once that trial is right."""
    scheduler = Scheduler([_one("A", target=5, drum=True), _one("B")], seed=1)
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)
    scheduler.expire()

    assert scheduler.ends is None
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)
    assert scheduler.ends is None
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.ends == TIME and not scheduler.done


def test_a_block_with_a_number_of_trials_ends_once_it_has_run_them_drummed_repeats_inside():
    """Engine spec §13.5's "a number of trials" (the PI, 2026-10-07), counted as the PI counts
    trials (2026-10-18: "200 trials; drummed repeats inside them"): a trial and its drummed repeats
    one, an ordinary repeat a trial of its own; the targets are a minimum, and past them every
    condition is drawn by its order."""
    block = Block(name="A", conditions=[Condition("x", {}, target=1), Condition("y", {}, target=1)],
                  trials=4, drum=True)
    scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=1)
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, Outcome.CORRECT)

    assert scheduler.block_trials == 2 and scheduler.past_targets and not scheduler.finished
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.block_trials == 3 and not scheduler.finished
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.finished and scheduler.ends == RULE and scheduler.done


def test_a_person_moves_a_run_on_a_block_or_back_one_and_it_is_recorded_with_who():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "'Next' and 'back one'": both directions by hand, each recorded with
    who; back one starts that block fresh; past the plan's last block, or before its first, nothing
    moves and the sentence says why."""
    scheduler = _stages()
    assert scheduler.move(BACK, by="kim") == (
        "block 'S1' is the first of the run's blocks, so there is no block before it")
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT)

    assert scheduler.move(NEXT, by="kim") is None and scheduler.block.name == "S2"
    assert scheduler.move(NEXT, by="kim") is None and scheduler.block.name == "S3"
    assert scheduler.move(NEXT, by="kim") == (
        "block 'S3' is the last its plan gives, so there is no next block; stop ends the run")
    assert scheduler.move(BACK, by="kim") is None and scheduler.block.name == "S2"
    assert [(end.block, end.ended, end.by) for end in scheduler.ended] == [
        ("S1", NEXT, "kim"), ("S2", NEXT, "kim"), ("S3", BACK, "kim")]


def test_a_drumming_switch_as_a_cyclic_plans_last_block_ends_is_for_the_block_that_follows():
    """`drum`'s carry reads the block the plan's order gives next (`following`), not the next in
    the list: at a cyclic plan's last block that is its first again."""
    scheduler = Scheduler([_one("A", drum=True), _one("B")], seed=1, order=Cyclic())
    scheduler.advance(RULE)
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT)

    assert scheduler.drumming_switched == "block", "A, which follows, drums by its declaration"
    assert scheduler.drum("off") == "A"


def test_a_plan_that_has_opened_every_block_type_since_its_last_trial_has_stalled():
    """The per-advance progress check (engine spec §13.5): a cyclic plan whose every block is over
    before it draws a trial would advance forever; it has stalled once it has opened each type
    since its last trial. A plan with one such type among others that draw has not."""
    empty = [_one("A", target=0), _one("B", target=0)]
    scheduler = Scheduler(empty, seed=1, order=Cyclic())
    assert scheduler.ends == RULE and not scheduler.stalled
    scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends)
    assert scheduler.ends == RULE and scheduler.stalled

    mixed = Scheduler([_one("A", target=0), _one("B")], seed=2, order=Random())
    for _ in range(12):
        if mixed.ends is None:
            break
        assert not mixed.stalled
        mixed.advance(mixed.ends)
    assert mixed.block.name == "B" and mixed.drawable


def test_a_persons_move_starts_the_progress_check_afresh():
    """The engine C2b plan's engineering review, I1 (its fuzz's seed 167): a fixed plan whose first
    block owes nothing and whose second owes three; a person moves back one before the second's
    first trial, and the first ends on its rule again. The plan has opened both types since its
    last trial, but a move is not the plan spinning: it has not stalled, and the second block
    draws."""
    scheduler = Scheduler([_one("A", target=0), _one("B", target=3)], seed=1)
    assert scheduler.ends == RULE and not scheduler.stalled
    scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends)
    assert scheduler.move(BACK, by="kim") is None and scheduler.block.name == "A"

    assert scheduler.ends == RULE and not scheduler.stalled
    assert scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends).name == "B" and scheduler.drawable


def test_a_condition_of_weight_0_is_never_drawn_and_leaves_nothing_to_draw_once_the_rest_are_paid():
    """The engine C2b plan's engineering review, I2: a weighted draw never gives a condition of
    weight 0, and once every other condition is paid there is nothing to draw -- `drawable` says
    so, for the loop to end the run with its sentence (the PI, 2026-10-19, "End run with a
    sentence") -- where `random.choices` raised. The draws before are the ones the seed gives
    with the condition left out."""
    def block(weights) -> Block:
        return Block(name="A", conditions=[Condition(c, {}, target=t) for c, t in
                                           (("a", 2), ("b", 3), ("c", 3))][:len(weights)],
                     order=WithReplacement(weights=weights))

    zero = Scheduler([block({"a": 0.0, "b": 1.0, "c": 2.0})], seed=4)
    drawn = []
    while zero.drawable:
        drawn.append(zero.next_trial().name)
        zero.record(drawn[-1], Outcome.CORRECT)

    assert sorted(drawn) == ["b"] * 3 + ["c"] * 3
    assert zero.owed("a") == 2 and zero.ends is None and not zero.drawable


def _second_stage(drum: bool = True, **stage) -> Scheduler:
    """A training progression at its second stage, drumming unless told not to, under a fall-back
    rule of under half of the last four answers right; the first stage passed on one right
    answer."""
    blocks = [_one("S1", target=1),
              Block(name="S2", conditions=[Condition("s2", {}, target=50)], drum=drum, **stage)]
    scheduler = Scheduler(blocks, seed=1, order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 4)))
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT)
    scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends)
    return scheduler


@pytest.mark.parametrize("criterion", [(1.0, 3), (0.8, 5), None])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("first_answers", [False, True])
def test_moving_back_judges_each_trials_first_answer_only_whatever_the_stage_counts(
        criterion, first_answers):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "First answer only": "Only each trial's first answer counts toward moving
    back, so one stubborn trial can't demote a stage the animal is otherwise doing well in" --
    under a streak, a percentage or no rule, with the training setting "first answers only" on or
    off (the science review, C1: the window sat in the stage rule's branch). Right first time
    twice, then one trial right only at its fourth try, then one at its second: four first answers,
    half right, not under half; one more wrong first answer and the stage falls back, once its
    drummed trial is right."""
    scheduler = _second_stage(criterion=criterion)
    scheduler.first_answers = first_answers
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT)
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET,
            Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.ends is None, "three first answers, two right: not judged yet"
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.ends is None, "four first answers, half right: not under half"

    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)
    assert scheduler.ends is None, "its drummed trial comes back first"
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.ends == FALL_BACK
    assert scheduler.because(FALL_BACK) == (
        "fell back: 1 of its last 4 first answers right, under 50%")


def test_a_broken_hold_a_stage_scores_counts_toward_moving_back_as_toward_moving_up():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Yes, the same": "A hold-training stage judges holds both ways: broken
    holds count toward moving up and toward moving back." A break the stage's own table does not
    score counts toward neither."""
    scored = _second_stage(drum=False, counting=Counting(
        after_stimulus=OWED, outcomes={"fixation_break": COUNTED}))
    plain = _second_stage(drum=False)
    for scheduler in (scored, plain):
        _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT, *[Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK] * 3)

    assert list(scored._back) == [1, 0, 0, 0] and scored.ends == FALL_BACK
    assert list(plain._back) == [1] and plain.ends is None


def test_a_balanced_plans_rounds_never_put_one_block_type_back_to_back():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Never back-to-back": "Each new round starts with a different type than
    the last one played, so every block boundary is a real switch; still balanced in counts." Over
    many seeds and two to five types, no two blocks in a row share a type, and each whole round
    still shows every type once."""
    for n in range(2, 6):
        blocks = [_one(f"T{i}") for i in range(n)]
        for seed in range(40):
            order = _run_out(Scheduler(blocks, seed=seed, order=Balanced(blocks=4 * n + 1)),
                             limit=400)
            assert all(a != b for a, b in zip(order, order[1:])), (n, seed, order)
            rounds = [sorted(order[i:i + n]) for i in range(0, 4 * n, n)]
            assert rounds == [sorted(b.name for b in blocks)] * 4, (n, seed)


def test_each_block_end_records_its_place_its_number_in_the_stream_and_when_a_top_stage_passed():
    """The engineering review, I4: each `BlockEnd` names its place in the run's sequence and the
    number the loop gave it as its first trial opened it in the stream, `None` for a block that
    ran no trial. The science review, I1: a top stage's end says after which trial its rule was
    first met, its animal working on past it."""
    scheduler = _stages(fall_back=None)
    scheduler.in_session = 7
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT)
    scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends)
    assert scheduler.move(NEXT, by="kim") is None
    scheduler.in_session = 9
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, *[Outcome.CORRECT] * 3)
    assert scheduler.passed_at is None, "not yet asked at a boundary"
    assert scheduler.ends is None and scheduler.passed_at == 7
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)
    scheduler.expire()
    assert scheduler.ends == TIME and scheduler.passed_at == 7
    scheduler.close(scheduler.ends)

    assert scheduler.ended == [BlockEnd("S1", RULE, None, 2, 0, 7, None),
                               BlockEnd("S2", NEXT, "kim", 2, 1, None, None),
                               BlockEnd("S3", TIME, None, 9, 2, 9, 7)]


def test_the_runs_time_closes_its_open_block_as_the_runs_recording_what_it_owed_so():
    """The science review, I3: the run's own time limit ending the run with a block open is
    recorded apart from the block's own time -- `RUN_TIME` -- and what the block still owed is
    recorded as not run for it, forgiving nothing."""
    scheduler = Scheduler([_one("A", target=3)], seed=1)
    _answer(scheduler, Outcome.CORRECT)
    scheduler.close(RUN_TIME)

    assert scheduler.shortfalls == [Shortfall("A", "a", 2, RUN_TIME, 0)]
    assert scheduler.ended == [BlockEnd("A", RUN_TIME, None, 0)]
    assert scheduler.owed("a") == 2


def test_each_block_end_is_said_with_its_numbers():
    """The science review, I2: one line on the changes feed per block end, with its reason and
    numbers -- "S2 -> S1: 8 of last 20 right"."""
    streak = _stages()
    _answer(streak, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT)
    percentage = Scheduler([_one("A", target=1, criterion=(0.8, 5), trials=6)], seed=1)
    _answer(percentage, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, *[Outcome.CORRECT] * 4)
    timed = Scheduler([_one("A", target=4)], seed=1)
    timed.expire()

    assert streak.because(RULE) == "its rule met: 3 right in a row, every target used up"
    assert percentage.because(RULE) == (
        "its rule met: 4 of its last 5 answers right, 6 trials run, every target used up")
    assert timed.because(TIME) == "its time ran out, its rule not met"
    assert timed.because(RUN_TIME) == "the run's time ran out"



def _fuzzed(seed: int) -> Scheduler:
    """A random plan: one to four block types of one to three conditions, targets 0 to 3, each
    perhaps a criterion, a number of trials, drumming and a condition order; and a random block
    order of each kind, the counted ones counted."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    blocks = []
    for b in range(rng.randint(1, 4)):
        order = rng.choice([Shuffled(), WithReplacement(weights={"c0": 2.0}),
                            Constrained(max_run=rng.randint(1, 2))])
        blocks.append(Block(
            name=f"B{b}",
            conditions=[Condition(f"c{c}", {}, target=rng.randint(0, 3))
                        for c in range(rng.randint(1, 3))],
            criterion=rng.choice([None, (rng.choice([0.5, 0.8, 1.0]), rng.randint(1, 4))]),
            trials=rng.choice([None, rng.randint(1, 6)]),
            drum=rng.random() < 0.3,
            order=order,
        ))
    order = rng.choice([Cyclic(cycles=rng.randint(1, 3)), Balanced(blocks=rng.randint(1, 6)),
                        Random(blocks=rng.randint(1, 6)),
                        ByCriterion(fall_back=rng.choice([None, (0.5, rng.randint(1, 4))]))])
    return Scheduler(blocks, seed=seed, counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED, cap=2),
                     order=order)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(0, 400, 40))
def test_no_random_plan_draws_from_nowhere_ends_a_block_on_a_drum_or_moves_a_stage_by_two(seed):
    """Engine build C2b's plans fuzzed, forty plans a case: whatever a plan's orders and end rules,
    a block that does not end can always draw (its targets, a repeat, a drum, or past its targets
    every condition); no block ends while a drum waits ("Finish the drum first"); a training
    progression moves one stage at a time; and every plan is done, stalls, or -- a progression's
    top stage working on -- runs until it is stopped."""
    for case in range(seed, seed + 40):
        scheduler = _fuzzed(case)
        rng = random.Random(case)
        for _ in range(800):
            if scheduler.done:
                break
            ending = scheduler.ends
            if ending is not None:
                assert scheduler._drum is None, case
                if scheduler.stalled:
                    break
                was = scheduler._position
                scheduler.advance(ending)
                if isinstance(scheduler.order, ByCriterion):
                    assert abs(scheduler._position - was) == 1, case
                continue
            assert scheduler.drawable, case
            if isinstance(scheduler.order, Balanced) and len(scheduler.blocks) > 1:
                sequence = scheduler._sequence
                assert all(a != b for a, b in zip(sequence, sequence[1:])), case
            name = scheduler.next_trial().name
            assert name in {c.name for c in scheduler.block.conditions}, case
            scheduler.record(name, rng.choice([Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT,
                                               Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK]))
            if rng.random() < 0.02:
                scheduler.expire()
        else:
            assert isinstance(scheduler.order, ByCriterion), case
```

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BlockEnd:
    """How one block of the run ended, for the run's end row (engine build C2b): its `block` type's
    name, why it `ended` (`RULE`, `TIME`, `FALL_BACK`, `NEXT`, `BACK`, `RUN_TIME` or `RUN`), `by`
    whom when a person moved it, and `after_trial`, the number of the last trial drawn before it
    ended, or `None` when the run drew none. **And where it stands** (the engine C2b plan's
    engineering review, I4): `position`, its place in the run's sequence of blocks, from 0 -- a
    training progression's stage -- and `block_in_session`, its number in the session's stream,
    the one its `BLOCK_START` carried, or `None` for a block that ran no trial, which the stream
    neither opens nor closes. **And `passed_at`**, at a training progression's top stage, the
    trial after which its rule was first met, its animal working on past it, or `None` (the
    science review, I1)."""

    block: str
    ended: str
    by: object = None
    after_trial: int | None = None
    position: int = 0
    block_in_session: int | None = None
    passed_at: int | None = None
```

```python
    def _type_at(self, position: int) -> int | None:
        """The block type the run's `position`th block is, by index into `blocks`, or `None` past
        the plan's end (engine spec §13.5): **in order once** (`Fixed`); **over and over**
        (`Cyclic`), its `cycles` passes; **in shuffled rounds** (`Balanced`: each round every type
        once, the last round partial where the count is not whole rounds); **each drawn at
        random** (`Random`); **stage by stage** (`ByCriterion`, which moves up and back by its
        rules, `_next_position`). A random sequence is drawn as far as it is read, from
        `_order_rng`, and kept, so the same position is always the same type; a balanced round
        never starts with the type the round before it ended with."""
        n = len(self.blocks)
        order = self.order
        if position < 0:
            return None
        if isinstance(order, Cyclic):
            if order.cycles is not None and position >= order.cycles * n:
                return None
            return position % n
        if isinstance(order, (Balanced, Random)):
            if order.blocks is not None and position >= order.blocks:
                return None
            while len(self._sequence) <= position:
                if isinstance(order, Balanced):
                    round_ = list(range(n))
                    self._order_rng.shuffle(round_)
                    if self._sequence and round_[0] == self._sequence[-1] and n > 1:
                        # **Never back-to-back** (the PI, 2026-10-19: "Each new round starts with
                        # a different type than the last one played, so every block boundary is a
                        # real switch; still balanced in counts"): the round turned by one, still
                        # a round, drawing nothing more from the seed.
                        round_ = round_[1:] + round_[:1]
                    self._sequence.extend(round_)
                else:
                    self._sequence.append(self._order_rng.randrange(n))
            return self._sequence[position]
        return position if position < n else None

    def _next_position(self, reason: str) -> int | None:
        """Where the run's sequence goes as the open block ends for `reason`: back one for a
        fall-back or a person's *back one*, else on one. `None` before the first."""
        if reason in (FALL_BACK, BACK):
            return self._position - 1 if self._position > 0 else None
        return self._position + 1

    def _fall_back_window(self) -> deque:
        rule = self.order.fall_back if isinstance(self.order, ByCriterion) else None
        return deque(maxlen=rule[1] if rule is not None and rule[1] >= 1 else 0)

    @property
    def _top(self) -> bool:
        """Whether the open block is a training progression's top stage (`ByCriterion`), where the
        animal keeps working once its rule is met (the PI, 2026-10-19, "Same, but top keeps
        going")."""
        return isinstance(self.order, ByCriterion) and self._position == len(self.blocks) - 1

    @property
    def _falls_back(self) -> bool:
        """Whether a training progression's fall-back rule is met (`ByCriterion.fall_back`): fewer
        than its proportion of the last window's answers right, judged once that many answers are
        in (the PI, 2026-10-19: "under 50% over the last 20", answers only) -- **each trial's first
        answer only** ("First answer only"; `record`) -- at any stage but the first, which has none
        below it."""
        if not isinstance(self.order, ByCriterion) or self.order.fall_back is None:
            return False
        proportion, window = self.order.fall_back
        return (self._position > 0 and window >= 1 and len(self._back) >= window
                and sum(self._back) / len(self._back) < proportion)

    @property
    def ends(self) -> str | None:
        """**Why the open block ends at this boundary**, or `None` while it goes on (engine spec
        §13.5; engine build C2b): `RULE` once its own end rule is met (`finished`) -- **but at a
        training progression's top stage the animal keeps working**, its conditions drawn again
        (`past_targets`); `FALL_BACK` once its plan's fall-back rule is met; `TIME` once its time
        has run out (`expire`). **Never while a drummed trial waits** (the PI, 2026-10-18 and
        2026-10-19: "Finish the drum first"): that trial comes back until it is right, then the
        block ends. A met rule counts before a fall-back -- at the top stage too, which works on
        while its rule holds -- and either before the time. **The top stage's rule met the first
        time is noted** (`passed_at`; the engine C2b plan's science review, I1): the record and
        the console say when it passed."""
        if self._drum is not None:
            return None
        if self.finished and not self._top:
            return RULE
        self._note_passed()
        if self._falls_back and not self.finished:
            return FALL_BACK
        if self._expired:
            return TIME
        return None

    def _note_passed(self) -> None:
        """Note the trial after which a training progression's top stage first met its rule
        (`passed_at`; the engine C2b plan's science review, I1), asked at every boundary (`ends`)
        and before every draw (`next_trial`), so it names the trial that met it whoever asks.
        `None` while it has not, or when it was met before the run's first trial."""
        if self.passed_at is None and self._top and self.finished:
            self.passed_at = self._number - 1 if self._number else None

    @property
    def following(self) -> Block | None:
        """The block the open one is followed by if it ends now (`ends`), or `None` while it goes on
        and when the plan is done then."""
        reason = self.ends
        if reason is None:
            return None
        position = self._next_position(reason)
        index = None if position is None else self._type_at(position)
        return None if index is None else self.blocks[index]

    @property
    def next_block(self) -> Block | None:
        """The block the plan goes on to when the open one ends on its own rule or its time -- the
        next stage up in a training progression -- or `None` where the plan ends there: what the
        console shows as next (engine build C2b)."""
        index = self._type_at(self._position + 1)
        return None if index is None else self.blocks[index]

    def expire(self) -> None:
        """The open block's time has run out (`Block.time_limit_s`; the PI, 2026-10-19), read by the
        loop between trials on the time trials ran, pauses not counting: it ends at this boundary,
        or once a drummed trial waiting is right (`ends`)."""
        self._expired = True

    @property
    def expired(self) -> bool:
        return self._expired

    @property
    def drum_waiting(self) -> str | None:
        """The condition whose drummed trial waits to come back until it is right, or `None`: what
        holds a block's end (`ends`) and, in the loop, the run's time limit (the PI, 2026-10-19,
        "Finish the drum first", for both), and what a person's move drops."""
        return None if self._drum is None else self._drum[0]

    def because(self, reason: str) -> str:
        """Why the open block ends for `reason`, in words and with its numbers, for the console's
        changes feed (the engine C2b plan's science review, I2: "S2 -> S1: 8 of last 20 right"):
        its rule and what met it, the fall-back and the first answers that met it, or whose time
        ran out."""
        if reason == RULE:
            met = []
            if self.block.criterion is not None:
                right = sum(self._window)
                met.append(f"{right} right in a row" if self.streak
                           else f"{right} of its last {len(self._window)} answers right")
            if self.block.trials is not None:
                met.append(f"{self.block_trials} trials run")
            return "its rule met: " + ", ".join(met + ["every target used up"])
        if reason == FALL_BACK:
            proportion = self.order.fall_back[0]
            return (f"fell back: {sum(self._back)} of its last {len(self._back)} first answers "
                    f"right, under {proportion:.0%}")
        if reason == TIME:
            return "its time ran out" + ("" if self.finished else ", its rule not met")
        if reason == RUN_TIME:
            return "the run's time ran out"
        return {NEXT: "moved on by hand", BACK: "moved back by hand"}.get(reason, "the run ended")

    @property
    def block_trials(self) -> int:
        """The open block's trials so far, a trial and its drummed repeats one: what its number of
        trials counts (`Block.trials`)."""
        return sum(counts.trials for counts in self._counts.values())

    @property
    def stalled(self) -> bool:
        """**The per-advance progress check** (engine spec §13.5: "`taskd`'s guard against a
        spinning plan becomes a per-advance progress check"; engine build C2b): since the last
        trial drawn, or a person's last move, the plan has opened every one of its block types and
        each ended before it drew a trial, so no block it can open will draw one and moving on again
        would spin. The run then ends, saying why (the PI, 2026-10-19, "End run with a
        sentence")."""
        return self._opened >= set(range(len(self.blocks)))

    @property
    def drawable(self) -> bool:
        """Whether the open block can draw a trial now: a drummed trial waits, a repeat waits, or a
        condition can be drawn by its order (`_peek`). A block that cannot, and does not end, has
        nothing to show (the PI, 2026-10-19: the run ends, saying why)."""
        return self._drum is not None or bool(self._due) or self._peek() is not None
```

```python
        self.in_session = None
        self.passed_at = None
        self._counts = {c.name: Counts() for c in self.block.conditions}
        self._queue.clear()
        self._window.clear()
        self._back = self._fall_back_window()
        self._drawn.clear()
        self._due.clear()
        self._drum = None
        self._expired = False
```

```python
    def close(self, reason: str, by: object = None) -> None:
        """Record how the open block ended (`BlockEnd`), once: by `advance`, or by the run as it
        ends with the block open. **A block its time ended writes what it still owed as not run**
        (the PI, 2026-10-19: "Trials it still owed are recorded as not run, 'time ran out'"): a
        `Shortfall` per condition owing, `because` `TIME` -- or `RUN_TIME`, the run's time --
        forgiving nothing."""
        if self.closed:
            return
        after = self._number - 1 if self._number else None
        if reason in (TIME, RUN_TIME):
            for c in self.block.conditions:
                owed = self.owed(c.name)
                if owed:
                    self.shortfalls.append(Shortfall(self.block.name, c.name, owed, reason, after))
        self.ended.append(BlockEnd(self.block.name, reason, by, after, self._position,
                                   self.in_session, self.passed_at))
        self.closed = True

    def move(self, step: str, by: object = None) -> str | None:
        """**A person moves the run on to the next block, or back one** (the PI, 2026-10-19,
        "'Next' and 'back one'": "Both directions by hand, each recorded with who"): `NEXT` to the
        block the plan's order gives next, the next stage up in a training progression; `BACK` to
        the block before the open one in the run's sequence, the stage below, started fresh. At
        once, a drummed trial waiting dropped. Returns why nothing moved -- there is no such block
        -- or `None`."""
        if step not in (NEXT, BACK):
            raise ValueError(f"a block is moved {NEXT} or {BACK}, not {step!r}")
        position = self._next_position(step)
        if position is None or self._type_at(position) is None:
            return (
                f"block {self.block.name!r} is the last its plan gives, so there is no next block; "
                f"stop ends the run"
                if step == NEXT
                else f"block {self.block.name!r} is the first of the run's blocks, so there is no "
                f"block before it"
            )
        self.advance(step, by)
        return None

```

```markdown
- **Basis:** PI decision; the named methods' primary sources. [@levitt1971transformed] — each transformed up-down rule converges where the probability of a "down" sequence is 0.5, so two-down/one-up converges on 70.7% (Sec. II.B, the derivation and Fig. 5, p. 472; Table I entry 2, p. 471); shrinking steps halve the step after the first, third, seventh, fifteenth, … runs (p. 471); "the presentations for two or more strategies can be interleaved at random", so the subject cannot anticipate (pp. 471-472). [@kaernbach1991simple] — weighted up-down with unequal steps can target any point on the psychometric function (abstract). [@garciaperez1998forcedchoice] — with fixed steps, where a staircase converges depends on the ratio of its steps (abstract; TSK-34's caveat). [@watson1983quest] — QUEST places each trial at the current most probable Bayesian estimate of threshold (abstract). [@watson2017quest] — QUEST+ (abstract). [@kontsevich1999bayesian] — Psi, the Bayesian estimate of threshold and slope by expected information gain (abstract). Reviews of adaptive procedures: [@treutwein1995adaptive] (abstract); [@leek2001adaptive] (abstract). Automated macaque training progressions: [@berger2018standardized] (abstract). The library-plus-code arrangement, per-animal state and the live hold are the PI's.
```

add

```markdown
- **Revised:** YYYY-MM-DD, built by engine build C2b as shuffled rounds within the run (TSK-58; `task.Balanced`).

### TSK-57 — A training progression moves up a stage when the stage's own rule is met, back one stage, started fresh, when the plan's fall-back rule is met (judged once its window of answers is in, on each trial's first answer only, a broken hold counting where the stage scores one), and keeps the animal working at the top stage; a stage whose time runs out moves on whether or not it passed, and the top stage's own time limit ends the run
- **Decided:** 2026-10-19, the PI: "Same, but top keeps going" -- move up "when the stage's own rule is met (e.g. 80% right over the last 20 answers, every condition shown its minimum)", back one stage, "starting it fresh, when a second rule the plan states is met (e.g. under 50% over the last 20), judged once that many answers are in", "but at the top stage the animal keeps working there until a time limit, a stop or the end of the session"; both rules judge answers only (his ruling of 2026-10-18, TSK-26); and, answering the plan's science review, "First answer only" -- "Only each trial's first answer counts toward moving back, so one stubborn trial can't demote a stage the animal is otherwise doing well in" -- and "Yes, the same" -- "A hold-training stage judges holds both ways: broken holds count toward moving up and toward moving back"; "Move on anyway" -- "Time-boxed stages: go to the next stage whether or not the animal passed." That the first stage, with none below it, does not fall back, that a met rule counts before a fall-back -- at the top stage for as long as its rule holds -- and a fall-back before a stage's time, and that a top stage's own time limit ends the run are the controller's readings and engineering ([the engine C2b plan](../superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-engine-c2b.md#appendix-b-for-the-pi-to-confirm)).
- **Where:** [engine spec §13](../superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-engine-design.md#13-structure-n10-nr1-nr6) item 5 and its note of YYYY-MM-DD; [`wl_xcon/task.py`](../../wl_xcon/task.py) (`ByCriterion`); [`wl_xcon/scheduler.py`](../../wl_xcon/scheduler.py) (`Scheduler.ends`).
- **Basis:** PI decision, with published precedent. [@asaad2008flexible] (Results, "Design") — blocks switch on a learning criterion judged by a moving average over the last n trials; [@nimhmonkeylogic2026task] — a block-change function "is called after every trial" ("Blocks"); [@berger2018standardized] — automated macaque training through "successively more complex" levels, task conditions updated from each animal's progress (abstract, the only part read). Falling back one stage and keeping the top stage have no published source we have read: engine spec §13 item 5 and §15 item 1 cite [@berger2018standardized] beside progressions that "may step back", which its abstract does not say (UNVERIFIED: its full text was not reachable when checked, 2026-10-08).

### TSK-58 — A plan's block order is fixed, cyclic, balanced in shuffled rounds within the run, never one type back-to-back where rounds meet, plain random, or a training progression; a random order is drawn from the run's recorded seed and replays
- **Decided:** 2026-10-07, the PI (TSK-25's four); 2026-10-11 (balanced within each run, TSK-56); 2026-10-19, the PI: "Shuffled rounds" -- "Each round shows every block type once, in a seeded random order. If the count isn't whole rounds, a warning at load says one type runs once more (chosen by the seed) and the run goes ahead; a run cut short leaves its last round incomplete, recorded."; "Both, as two choices" -- "A plan picks 'balanced' or 'random'; random can repeat a type and leave another out by chance, recorded with its seed."; and, answering the plan's science review, "Never back-to-back" -- "Each new round starts with a different type than the last one played, so every block boundary is a real switch; still balanced in counts." A cyclic plan's count of passes, or none, the random orders' own generator, apart from the conditions' so their draws are as the seed gives them, and a round that would start with the type just played turned by one, are engineering ([the engine C2b plan](../superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-engine-c2b.md#calls), call 5).
- **Where:** [engine spec §13](../superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-engine-design.md#13-structure-n10-nr1-nr6) item 5 and its note of YYYY-MM-DD; [`wl_xcon/task.py`](../../wl_xcon/task.py) (`Fixed`, `Cyclic`, `Balanced`, `Random`, `ByCriterion`); [`wl_xcon/scheduler.py`](../../wl_xcon/scheduler.py) (`Scheduler._type_at`).
- **Basis:** PI decision. Shuffled passes are an established ordering for conditions [@nimhmonkeylogic2026task] ("Blocks"; TSK-23); no published source for blocks balanced within a run.
```
