### Task 6: The load-time checks of plans (closes XC-381, XC-386 and XC-388)

Under the PI's rule of 2026-10-11 (decision 25; TSK-51), each check is a warning saying why, unless an authoring rule, the run's breaking or an unreadable record demands a refusal, said as such (call 15). `check._plan_faults`, appended to `check_task`, so every caller -- `wlx check`, `wlx run`, the pre-flight's task checks, `Session.run`'s backstop -- sees it. **Refused, `bad-plan`**: a plan of no blocks; a block's conditions not a list of conditions; two block types of one plan, or two conditions of one block, with one name (each is what a trial's row names it by, wl-preproc reading a block's type from its `block`; **XC-388**); a block of no conditions ending on a criterion or a number of trials, which can never end (**XC-381**, an `IndexError` at its first draw); a condition playing a structure the task lacks, setting a setting the task does not declare -- a bounded one, a reward size, among them (Appendix A, item 7) -- or setting it to a word where a number belongs or a choice not offered; a negative target; a criterion or a fall-back rule outside (0, 1] or over a window under 1 (a `ZeroDivisionError` at the first `finished`, Surprise 10); a number of trials, a count or a time limit that is no number of them; weights that are no weights. **Warned**, accepted in every kind of session: `late-condition` (**XC-386**: a criterion block's target-0 condition comes only after the others' targets); `endless-plan` (a cyclic, balanced or random plan of no count with no run time limit; **a training progression unless the plan has a run time limit, or it has no fall-back rule and every stage has a time limit** -- an animal that never meets an untimed stage's rule works on there, the top stage's included, and a fall-back can move a run of timed stages up and back forever: the science review, C3, where the drafted check let a timed top stage end it); `unbalanced-plan` (Q8's warning); `empty-block` (over before it starts); `block-table-unread` (a block's own table naming where its stimulus appears or that withholding is an answer, which nothing reads, Surprise 10); `no-run-limit` (`Constrained.max_run` under 1, which limits nothing); **`weightless-condition`** (a condition of weight 0 owed trials, which its order never draws, so its block cannot end on its rule and the run ends saying it can draw no further trial: the engineering review, I2, warned rather than refused under the PI's rule, since the run no longer faults -- Task 5); **`fall-back-unjudged`** (a fall-back rule on a plan of one stage, which has none below it, or at a stage with no criterion whose every answer pays, so it ends before the fall-back's window of first answers is in: the engineering review, Minor 3); **`fall-back-bounce`** (a fall-back bar at or above a stage's pass bar, so an animal that just passes it can be moved straight back; or a stage judging fewer answers than the fall-back, so it can pass while the fall-back's answers are under its bar: the science review, M2). A condition's value outside its range is the pre-flight's (Task 8).

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/check.py`
- Modify (docs): `docs/backlog.md` (XC-381, XC-386 and XC-388's lines go)
- Create (test): `tests/test_plan_checks.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `task.Plan` and its parts (Task 3).
- Produces: `check._plan_faults(task) -> list[Finding]`, called by `check_task`; finding codes `bad-plan` (blocking) and the warnings `late-condition`, `endless-plan`, `unbalanced-plan`, `empty-block`, `block-table-unread`, `no-run-limit`, `weightless-condition`, `fall-back-unjudged`, `fall-back-bounce`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/test_plan_checks.py`:

```python
"""The load-time checks of a task's plans (engine spec §13; engine build C2b).

Under the PI's rule of 2026-10-11, warnings over refusals: a plan is refused only for what cannot
run or would leave its record unreadable, each sentence saying which plan and block and what to
write, and warned, saying why, for what runs but may not do what was meant. Every caller that holds
a task to its checks -- `wlx check`, `wlx run`, the pre-flight, `Session.run`'s backstop -- sees
these through `check.check_task`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from wl_xcon.check import check_task
from wl_xcon.cli import _load_task
from wl_xcon.findings import SESSION_KINDS
from wl_xcon.scheduler import EVERY_TRIAL
from wl_xcon.task import (
    OWED,
    Balanced,
    Block,
    ByCriterion,
    Condition,
    Constrained,
    Counting,
    Cyclic,
    Plan,
    Random,
    WithReplacement,
)

BASE = _load_task(Path("tasks/fixation_detection.py"))
#: The codes this file's checks give; the reference task gives none of them.
PLAN_CODES = {"bad-plan", "endless-plan", "unbalanced-plan", "late-condition", "empty-block",
              "block-table-unread", "no-run-limit", "weightless-condition", "fall-back-unjudged",
              "fall-back-bounce"}

NEAR = Condition("near", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=4)
FAR = Condition("far", {"target_x": 12.0}, target=4, structure="detection")


def _findings(**plans) -> list:
    task = dataclasses.replace(BASE, plans=plans)
    return [f for f in check_task(task) if f.code in PLAN_CODES]


def test_a_plan_the_rig_can_run_adds_no_finding():
    """A balanced plan of whole rounds and a run time limit; a training progression with a fall-back
    rule and a run time limit; conditions naming the task's structure and setting its settings
    within their ranges."""
    blocks = [Block("near-far", [NEAR, FAR], criterion=(0.8, 10)),
              Block("far", [FAR], trials=20, time_limit_s=300.0,
                    order=WithReplacement(weights={"far": 2.0}))]
    stages = [Block("easy", [NEAR], criterion=(0.8, 10)),
              Block("hard", [FAR], criterion=(0.8, 10), time_limit_s=600.0)]

    assert _findings(
        balanced=Plan(blocks, order=Balanced(blocks=4), time_limit_s=1800.0),
        training=Plan(stages, order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 10)), time_limit_s=3600.0),
        fixed=Plan(blocks),
    ) == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("plan", "said"),
    [
        (Plan([]), "it has no blocks"),
        (Plan([Block("b", (NEAR,))]), "a block's conditions are a list of Conditions"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR]), Block("b", [FAR])]), "two of its block types are named 'b'"),
        # XC-388: two conditions of one block with one name.
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR, dataclasses.replace(FAR, name="near")])]),
         "block 'b' has two conditions named 'near'"),
        # XC-381: a block that can never end.
        (Plan([Block("b", [], criterion=(0.8, 10))]), "has no conditions and ends on a criterion"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [], trials=10)]), "has no conditions and ends on a number of trials"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [dataclasses.replace(NEAR, structure="search")])]),
         "plays structure 'search', which the task does not have"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [Condition("c", {"reward_correct": 0.2}, target=1)])]),
         "sets 'reward_correct', which is no setting the task declares"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [Condition("c", {"target_x": "far"}, target=1)])]),
         "sets 'target_x' to 'far', and it takes a number (deg)"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [Condition("c", {"target_appearance": "disc"}, target=1)])]),
         "sets 'target_appearance' to 'disc', and it is one of"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [dataclasses.replace(NEAR, target=-1)])]), "has target -1"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR], criterion=(0.8, 0))]), "criterion is (0.8, 0)"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR], criterion=(1.5, 10))]), "criterion is (1.5, 10)"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR])], order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 0)), time_limit_s=60.0),
         "its fall-back rule is (0.5, 0)"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR], trials=0)]), "number of trials is 0"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR], time_limit_s=0.0)]), "time limit is 0.0, a number of seconds"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR])], time_limit_s=-5.0), "its time limit is -5.0"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR])], order=Cyclic(cycles=0)), "its order's count is 0"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR], order=WithReplacement(weights={"near": -1.0}))]),
         "weights are {'near': -1.0}"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR], order=WithReplacement(weights={"near": 0.0}))]),
         "at least one condition's above 0"),
    ],
)
def test_a_plan_that_cannot_run_is_refused_saying_which_and_why(plan, said):
    """Unrunnable, or unreadable in the record: refused in every kind of session, as an authoring
    rule, never a warning (the PI's rule of 2026-10-11 keeps refusals for these)."""
    found = _findings(p=plan)

    assert [f.code for f in found] == ["bad-plan"], found
    assert found[0].detail.startswith("plan 'p': ") and said in found[0].detail
    assert all(found[0].refuses(kind) for kind in SESSION_KINDS)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("plan", "code", "said"),
    [
        # XC-386: a target-0 condition beside nonzero targets in a criterion block.
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR, dataclasses.replace(FAR, target=0)], criterion=(0.8, 10))]),
         "late-condition", "condition(s) 'far' have target 0"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR])], order=Cyclic()), "endless-plan",
         "count of none repeats its blocks"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR])], order=Random()), "endless-plan", "no time limit"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR], criterion=(0.8, 10))], order=ByCriterion()), "endless-plan",
         "never meets the rule of stage 'b', which has no time limit"),
        (Plan([Block("a", [NEAR]), Block("b", [FAR])], order=Balanced(blocks=5)),
         "unbalanced-plan", "5 blocks of 2 block types are not whole rounds"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [dataclasses.replace(NEAR, target=0)]), Block("c", [FAR])]),
         "empty-block", "block 'b' owes no trial -- every target 0"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR], counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED, appears="stim_on"))]),
         "block-table-unread", "its stimulus appears in 'stim_on'"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR], counting=dataclasses.replace(EVERY_TRIAL, withholding=True))]),
         "block-table-unread", "withholding is an answer"),
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR], order=Constrained(max_run=0))]), "no-run-limit",
         "longest run is 0"),
        # The engineering review, I2: a condition of weight 0 owed trials is never drawn.
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR, FAR], order=WithReplacement(weights={"near": 0.0}))]),
         "weightless-condition", "condition(s) 'near' weigh 0"),
        # Minor 3: a fall-back rule never judged -- one stage, or a stage over before its window.
        (Plan([Block("b", [NEAR], criterion=(0.8, 10))], order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 10)),
              time_limit_s=600.0),
         "fall-back-unjudged", "it has one stage and a fall-back rule"),
        (Plan([Block("easy", [NEAR], criterion=(0.8, 10)), Block("mid", [NEAR]),
               Block("hard", [FAR], criterion=(0.8, 10))],
              order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 10)), time_limit_s=600.0),
         "fall-back-unjudged", "stage 'mid' has no criterion and every answer pays its condition, "
         "so it ends on its rule after 4 first answers at most"),
        # The science review, M2: a progression prone to move up and back.
        (Plan([Block("a", [NEAR], criterion=(0.6, 10)), Block("b", [FAR], criterion=(0.8, 10))],
              order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.7, 10)), time_limit_s=600.0),
         "fall-back-bounce", "under 70% of the last 10 first answers right, is at or above stage "
         "'a''s bar of 60%"),
        (Plan([Block("a", [NEAR], criterion=(0.8, 10)), Block("b", [FAR], criterion=(0.8, 5)),
               Block("c", [NEAR], criterion=(0.8, 10))],
              order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 10)), time_limit_s=600.0),
         "fall-back-bounce", "stage 'b' judges its last 5 answers and the fall-back rule the last "
         "10 first answers"),
    ],
)
def test_a_plan_that_runs_but_may_not_do_what_was_meant_is_a_warning_saying_why(plan, code, said):
    """The PI, 2026-10-11: "warnings are more appropriate most of the time with an explanation of
    why it may not be a good idea": each accepted in every kind of session, refusing none."""
    found = _findings(p=plan)

    assert [f.code for f in found] == [code], found
    assert said in found[0].detail
    assert not found[0].blocking and set(found[0].accepted_in) == set(SESSION_KINDS)


def test_a_training_progression_ends_of_itself_only_with_every_stage_timed_and_no_fall_back():
    """The science review, C3: a training progression is warned as endless unless the plan has a
    run time limit, or it has no fall-back rule and every stage has a time limit -- an animal that
    never meets an untimed stage's rule works on there, the top stage's included, and a fall-back
    can move a run of timed stages up and back forever."""
    stages = [Block("easy", [NEAR], criterion=(0.8, 10)), Block("hard", [FAR], criterion=(0.8, 10))]
    top_timed = [stages[0], dataclasses.replace(stages[1], time_limit_s=600.0)]
    all_timed = [dataclasses.replace(stage, time_limit_s=600.0) for stage in stages]

    def codes(blocks, **plan) -> list:
        return [f.code for f in _findings(p=Plan(blocks, **plan))]

    assert codes(stages, order=ByCriterion()) == ["endless-plan"]
    assert codes(top_timed, order=ByCriterion()) == ["endless-plan"]
    assert codes(all_timed, order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 10))) == ["endless-plan"]
    assert "forever" in _findings(
        p=Plan(all_timed, order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 10))))[0].detail
    assert codes(all_timed, order=ByCriterion()) == []
    assert codes(stages, order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 10)), time_limit_s=1800.0) == []
```

```python

def _plan_faults(task: Task) -> list[Finding]:
    """**The load-time checks of a task's plans** (engine spec §13; engine build C2b), under the
    PI's rule of warnings over refusals (2026-10-11: "warnings are more appropriate most of the
    time with an explanation of why ... the experimenter needs free control most of the time").
    **Refused**, `bad-plan`, each sentence saying which plan and block and what to write, only
    what cannot run or would leave the record unreadable: a plan of no blocks; two block types of
    one plan, or two conditions of one block, with one name (each is what a trial's row names it
    by; XC-388); a condition playing a structure the task lacks, or setting a value the task
    cannot take; a block that can never end -- a criterion or a number of trials over no
    conditions (XC-381); a criterion, a fall-back rule, a number of trials, a count or a time
    limit that is no rule at all; weights that are no weights. **Warned**, accepted in every kind
    of session, what runs but may not do what was meant, each saying why: a criterion block
    mixing a target-0 condition with others (XC-386); a plan with no end of its own and no run
    time limit -- a training progression's own end only where every stage has a time limit and no
    fall-back rule moves it back (the plan's science review, C3); a balanced count that is not
    whole rounds (the PI, 2026-10-19); a block over before it starts; a block's own table saying
    where its stimulus appears or whether withholding is an answer, which only a structure says;
    a run limit of none; a condition of weight 0 owed trials, which is never drawn (the
    engineering review, I2); a fall-back rule never judged -- on a plan of one stage, or at a
    stage that always ends before its window is in (Minor 3); and a progression prone to move up
    and back between two stages (the science review, M2). A condition's value outside its range
    is the pre-flight's to re-check widened (`preflight.beyond`)."""
    findings: list[Finding] = []
    declared = {param.name: param for param in task.params}

    def most_answers(block) -> int | None:
        """The most first answers a stage with no criterion can be judged on before its rule ends
        it, however the animal does: where every answer pays its condition -- each table it counts
        by classing every answer counted or spent, so a wrong one is never shown again -- it ends
        once its targets are used up, or its number of trials run if that is more. `None` where a
        criterion, or an answer that does not pay, can keep it going."""
        if block.criterion is not None:
            return None
        for condition in block.conditions:
            structure = task.structures.get(condition.structure or task.default)
            table = block.counting if block.counting is not None else (
                None if structure is None else structure.counting)
            if table is None:
                return None
            answers = set(ANSWERS)
            if block.counting is None and structure.counting.withholding:
                answers.add(Outcome.NO_RESPONSE)
            if any(table.of(answer, True) not in (COUNTED, SPENT) for answer in answers):
                return None
        targets = sum(c.target for c in block.conditions if isinstance(c.target, int))
        return max(targets, block.trials if isinstance(block.trials, int) else 0)

    def refuse(plan: str, said: str) -> None:
        findings.append(Finding("bad-plan", f"plan {plan!r}: {said}"))

    def warn(code: str, plan: str, said: str) -> None:
        findings.append(Finding(code, f"plan {plan!r}: {said}", blocking=False,
                                accepted_in=SESSION_KINDS))

    def rule(value: object) -> bool:
        """Whether `value` is `(proportion, window)`, a proportion in (0, 1] and a whole window."""
        return (isinstance(value, tuple) and len(value) == 2 and _finite(value[0])
                and 0 < value[0] <= 1 and _is_number(value[1]) and isinstance(value[1], int)
                and value[1] >= 1)

    def count(value: object) -> bool:
        return value is None or (isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)
                                 and value >= 1)

    def seconds(value: object) -> bool:
        return value is None or (_finite(value) and value > 0)

    for name, plan in task.plans.items():
        order = plan.order
        if not plan.blocks:
            refuse(name, "it has no blocks, so a run of it would have nothing to play; give it "
                         "blocks=[Block(...), ...]")
            continue
        names = [block.name for block in plan.blocks]
        for twice in sorted({n for n in names if names.count(n) > 1}):
            refuse(name, f"two of its block types are named {twice!r}, and each trial's row names "
                         f"its block's type by its name (wl-preproc reads it so); name each once")
        if not seconds(plan.time_limit_s):
            refuse(name, f"its time limit is {plan.time_limit_s!r}, and a run's time limit is a "
                         f"number of seconds above zero, or None")
        counted = getattr(order, "cycles", None) if isinstance(order, Cyclic) else getattr(
            order, "blocks", None)
        if not count(counted):
            refuse(name, f"its order's count is {counted!r}, and a count of blocks or cycles is a "
                         f"whole number from 1, or None for no count")
        fall_back = order.fall_back if isinstance(order, ByCriterion) else None
        if fall_back is not None and not rule(fall_back):
            refuse(name, f"its fall-back rule is {fall_back!r}, and a rule is (proportion, "
                         f"window): a proportion above 0 and at most 1, over a window of 1 or "
                         f"more answers")
        untimed = [block.name for block in plan.blocks if block.time_limit_s is None]
        if isinstance(order, ByCriterion):
            # **A training progression ends of itself only where every stage has a time limit and
            # no fall-back rule moves it back** (the plan's science review, C3): an animal that
            # never meets an untimed stage's rule works on there, and a fall-back can move a run of
            # timed stages up and back forever.
            why = (f"an animal that never meets the rule of stage {untimed[0]!r}, which has no "
                   f"time limit, works on there" if untimed
                   else "its fall-back rule can move a run up and back between its timed stages "
                        "forever")
            endless = bool(untimed) or fall_back is not None
        else:
            why = "a count of none repeats its blocks"
            endless = isinstance(order, (Cyclic, Balanced, Random)) and counted is None
        if endless and plan.time_limit_s is None:
            warn("endless-plan", name, (
                "it has no end of its own and no time limit, so a run of it goes on until a "
                f"person stops it, a scheduled stop or the out-of-cage limit ends it ({why}); give "
                "the plan time_limit_s= to end it sooner"))
        if fall_back is not None and rule(fall_back):
            proportion, window = fall_back
            if len(plan.blocks) == 1:
                warn("fall-back-unjudged", name, (
                    "it has one stage and a fall-back rule, and the first stage has no stage below "
                    "it to fall back to, so the rule is never applied; give the plan another "
                    "stage, or no fall_back"))
            for block in plan.blocks[1:-1]:
                most = most_answers(block)
                if most is not None and most < window:
                    warn("fall-back-unjudged", name, (
                        f"stage {block.name!r} has no criterion and every answer pays its "
                        f"condition, so it ends on its rule after {most} first answers at most, "
                        f"and the fall-back rule, judged once {window} first answers are in, is "
                        f"never judged there"))
            for at, block in enumerate(plan.blocks[:-1]):
                if block.criterion is None or not rule(block.criterion):
                    continue
                bar, judged = block.criterion
                if proportion >= bar:
                    warn("fall-back-bounce", name, (
                        f"its fall-back rule, under {proportion:.0%} of the last {window} first "
                        f"answers right, is at or above stage {block.name!r}'s bar of "
                        f"{bar:.0%}, so an animal that just passes {block.name!r} can be moved "
                        f"back from the stage above at the rate that passed it, up and back "
                        f"again"))
                if at >= 1 and judged < window:
                    warn("fall-back-bounce", name, (
                        f"stage {block.name!r} judges its last {judged} answers and the fall-back "
                        f"rule the last {window} first answers, so it can be passed while the "
                        f"fall-back's are under its bar -- a move up counts first -- and the stage "
                        f"above can move the animal straight back"))
        if isinstance(order, Balanced) and counted is not None and counted % len(plan.blocks):
            warn("unbalanced-plan", name, (
                f"{counted} blocks of {len(plan.blocks)} block types are not whole rounds, so its "
                f"last round shows only {counted % len(plan.blocks)} of them, chosen by the run's "
                f"seed, and they run once more than the rest"))
        for block in plan.blocks:
            where = f"block {block.name!r}"
            if not isinstance(block.conditions, list) or not all(
                    isinstance(c, Condition) for c in block.conditions):
                refuse(name, f"{where}'s conditions are {block.conditions!r}, and a block's "
                             f"conditions are a list of Conditions")
                continue
            conditions = list(block.conditions)
            named = [c.name for c in conditions]
            for twice in sorted({n for n in named if named.count(n) > 1}):
                refuse(name, f"{where} has two conditions named {twice!r}: they would share one "
                             f"count, and each trial's row names its condition by its name "
                             f"(XC-388); name each once")
            if not conditions and (block.criterion is not None or block.trials is not None):
                rule_ = "a criterion" if block.criterion is not None else "a number of trials"
                refuse(name, f"{where} has no conditions and ends on {rule_}, which a block that "
                             f"draws no trial can never meet (XC-381); give it conditions")
            if block.criterion is not None and not rule(block.criterion):
                refuse(name, f"{where}'s criterion is {block.criterion!r}, and a criterion is "
                             f"(proportion, window): a proportion above 0 and at most 1, over a "
                             f"window of 1 or more answers")
            if not count(block.trials):
                refuse(name, f"{where}'s number of trials is {block.trials!r}, a whole number from "
                             f"1, or None")
            if not seconds(block.time_limit_s):
                refuse(name, f"{where}'s time limit is {block.time_limit_s!r}, a number of seconds "
                             f"above zero, or None")
            weights = getattr(block.order, "weights", {})
            if any(not _finite(w) or w < 0 for w in weights.values()) or (
                    weights and conditions
                    and not any(weights.get(c.name, 1.0) > 0 for c in conditions)):
                refuse(name, f"{where}'s weights are {weights!r}: each a number of 0 or more, at "
                             f"least one condition's above 0")
            else:
                never = [c.name for c in conditions if weights.get(c.name, 1.0) == 0
                         and isinstance(c.target, int) and c.target > 0]
                if never:
                    # The engine C2b plan's engineering review, I2: a weight of 0 is never drawn.
                    warn("weightless-condition", name, (
                        f"{where}'s condition(s) {', '.join(map(repr, never))} weigh 0, so its "
                        f"order never draws them, and they are owed trials: a block of it cannot "
                        f"use up its targets and end on its rule, and once the others are paid a "
                        f"run of it ends, saying it can draw no further trial; give each a weight "
                        f"above 0, or a target of 0"))
            if isinstance(block.order, Constrained) and (
                    not isinstance(block.order.max_run, int) or block.order.max_run < 1):
                warn("no-run-limit", name, (
                    f"{where}'s longest run is {block.order.max_run!r}, which limits no run of one "
                    f"condition; give it 1 or more"))
            for condition in conditions:
                of = f"{where}'s condition {condition.name!r}"
                if (isinstance(condition.target, bool) or not isinstance(condition.target, int)
                        or condition.target < 0):
                    refuse(name, f"{of} has target {condition.target!r}, a whole number of 0 or "
                                 f"more")
                if condition.structure is not None and condition.structure not in task.structures:
                    refuse(name, f"{of} plays structure {condition.structure!r}, which the task "
                                 f"does not have ({', '.join(map(repr, task.structures))})")
                values = condition.values if isinstance(condition.values, dict) else {}
                for setting, value in values.items():
                    param = declared.get(setting)
                    if param is None:
                        refuse(name, f"{of} sets {setting!r}, which is no setting the task "
                                     f"declares; a condition sets the task's own settings")
                    elif param.choices and value not in param.choices:
                        refuse(name, f"{of} sets {setting!r} to {value!r}, and it is one of "
                                     f"{param.choices}")
                    elif not param.choices and not _finite(value):
                        refuse(name, f"{of} sets {setting!r} to {value!r}, and it takes a number "
                                     f"({param.unit})")
            targets = [c.target for c in conditions if isinstance(c.target, int)]
            if block.criterion is not None and 0 in targets and any(targets):
                zero = ", ".join(repr(c.name) for c in conditions if c.target == 0)
                warn("late-condition", name, (
                    f"{where} ends on a criterion and its condition(s) {zero} have target 0 beside "
                    f"others owed trials: a condition is drawn only while it is owed, or once "
                    f"every target is used up, so {zero} come only after the others' targets "
                    f"(XC-386)"))
            if (block.criterion is None and block.trials is None
                    and all(c.target == 0 for c in conditions)):
                none = "no conditions" if not conditions else "every target 0"
                warn("empty-block", name, (
                    f"{where} owes no trial -- {none} -- and has no criterion or number of trials, "
                    f"so a block of it ends before it draws a trial"))
            own = block.counting
            if own is not None and (own.appears is not None or own.withholding):
                warn("block-table-unread", name, (
                    f"{where}'s own counting table says "
                    + " and ".join(said for said, on in (
                        (f"its stimulus appears in {own.appears!r}", own.appears is not None),
                        ("withholding is an answer", own.withholding)) if on)
                    + ", which only a structure says: where its stimulus appears and what is an "
                      "answer are each condition's structure's, and the block's are not read"))
    return findings

```

```markdown
- **XC-381** A block that ends on a criterion and has no conditions loads, and faults with an IndexError at its first draw (`Scheduler.next_trial`: no condition left to draw); refuse it at load with a sentence. — 2026-10-18, [engine C2a plan, "Not in C2a"](superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-engine-c2a.md#not-in-c2a) — waits on: engine build C2b (the load-time checks of plans)
- **XC-388** Nothing refuses two conditions of one block with the same name: `_counts` merges them by name, `owed()` takes the first one's target and `past_targets` each one's own, so they disagree; C2b's load-time plan checks refuse it. — 2026-10-18, [engine C2a plan, "Not in C2a"](superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-engine-c2a.md#not-in-c2a) (the final review, minor) — waits on: engine build C2b (plans)
- **XC-386** A block that ends on a criterion and mixes a target-0 condition with nonzero targets holds the target-0 condition back until the others are paid, then draws it; C2b's load-time checks could warn on it. — 2026-10-18, [engine C2a plan, "Not in C2a"](superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-engine-c2a.md#not-in-c2a) (Task 13's review, minor 4) — waits on: engine build C2b (plans)
```

---

### Task 7: A run plays its plan: time limits in the loop, the progress check, and `wlx run --plan` (closes XC-207)

**The run plays the plan it names** (decisions 18 and 21): `RunSpec.plan` and `SessionSpec.plan` name one of the task's plans; `Session._plan(run, task)` returns it -- or blocks given in code, a test's, or the plain run's one block -- as a `task.Plan`, whose blocks and order the scheduler is built with. A plan the task lacks is refused before the run starts, the backstop for a caller that took no pre-flight. **The time limits are read in the loop between trials, never in `Session._ends`** (scope R25; calls 6 and 8): after the out-of-cage limit and the scheduled stop (`_ends`, untouched) and the pause (`_hold`, untouched), the block's own limit first -- reached, `expire`, and the block ends at this boundary, or once a drummed trial waiting is right (Q1, Q2) -- then the run's: reached **with no drummed trial waiting** (answer 15, "Finish the drum first", for a run's limit too), the open block closes **`rule` if its rule is met at this boundary, else `run_time`** (the science review, I3: the drafted loop read the run's limit first, so a stage passed on the run's last trial was recorded `time`), its owed trials recorded as not run, and the run ends `completed` with its sentence (Q5), never `limit`. **Both on the trial clock, `Session._elapsed`**, each trial's frames at the frame period and its ITI, which a pause does not move (Q6). **The progress check replaces the counted spin guard** (Q7; call 7): a plan that has opened every block type since its last trial, each ending before it drew one, ends the run with a sentence, as does a block that cannot draw and does not end (where `next_trial` raised `IndexError`, a fault); `completed`, the session going on. The counted guard raised `RuntimeError` once a run advanced as many times as its plan has blocks, which a cyclic or falling-back plan does by design (Surprise 4). **The record**: the start row's `plan` and **`plan_form`**, the plan as declared in the content hash's canonical form (the science review, I4), a planned run's `trials` null and `blocks` its plan's block types as declared; the end row's `block_ends`, how each block ended, **with its `position` and its `block_in_session`** -- the loop gives the scheduler each block's stream number as its first trial opens it, so an entry joins the stream's block, null for a block that ran no trial (the engineering review, I4) -- and a top stage's `passed_at`; and **`end_reason`**, why a completed run ended: `plan_done`, `plan_time`, `stalled` or `cannot_draw` (the science review, I3). **The console hears each move to another block**: one line on the changes feed, its reason and numbers (`Scheduler.because`; the science review, I2), a block the run ends with said by the run's stop reason. A time is said in whole minutes and seconds (`_minutes`; the engineering review, Minor 4). **`wlx run --plan NAME`** (call 13), refused before the session opens when the task lacks it. **A condition may set a setting that holds a whole position to one of its `Polar` choices**, and its trials play it and record it polar first (Task 2's writer; decision 17). The comments that said a run is one block "until a task program or the rig gives them" -- `scheduler.py`'s, `_plan`'s, `SessionSpec.blocks`', `encode.py`'s `CONDITION` and its test's -- are made true (call 24). The docs record plans running (architecture.md; the engine spec §13's note, which also holds `berger2018standardized` to what its abstract states -- the science review, M6; S8 §1 amended; TSK-25; TSK-59 to TSK-61), and the backlog closes XC-207, re-points XC-197 to the registry (Q11), drops XC-207 from XC-013's wait, narrows XC-208, and **files XC-406**, measuring on a rig how far the nominal trial clock runs from the wall (the engineering review, Minor 1), "Next free" raised to XC-407.

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/taskd.py`, `wl_xcon/scheduler.py` (its module docstring), `wl_xcon/cli.py`, `wl_xcon/encode.py`
- Modify (docs): `docs/design/architecture.md`, the engine spec (§13's note), the S8 spec (§1), `docs/references/decisions.md` (TSK-25; new TSK-59, TSK-60, TSK-61), `docs/backlog.md`
- Modify (test): `tests/test_taskd.py`, `tests/test_cli.py`, `tests/test_event_encoding.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Scheduler(order=...)`, `ends`, `following`, `expire`, `expired`, `stalled`, `drawable`, `advance(reason)`, `close`, `ended`, `in_session`, `drum_waiting`, `because`, `RUN_TIME` (Task 5); `task.Plan` (Task 3).
- Produces: `RunSpec.plan`, `SessionSpec.plan: str | None = None`; `Session._plan(run, task) -> Plan`; `Session._run_from`, `_block_from`, `_played`; `taskd.END_REASONS`, `_minutes(seconds)`; the start row's `plan` and `plan_form`, the end row's `block_ends` (`{block, ended, by, after_trial, block_in_session, position, passed_at}`) and `end_reason`; the changes feed's `block` lines; `wlx run --plan NAME`.

```python

# --- A run plays the plan it names (engine build C2b) -----------------------------------------

#: Two block types of `fixation_detection`'s one condition each, near then far.
_TWO = ('[Block("near", [Condition("near", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=2)]), '
        'Block("far", [Condition("far", {"target_x": 12.0}, target=2)])]')


def _planned(tmp_path, plans: str, *, plan: str, link=None, **spec) -> Session:
    path = _sessions.planned_task(tmp_path, plans)
    return _session(_spec(tmp_path, task=str(path), plan=plan, trials=400, **spec), link=link)


def test_a_run_plays_the_plan_it_names_and_its_rows_record_it(tmp_path):
    """Engine spec §13.1 (the PI, 2026-10-07: "the operator chooses at run start among the plans it
    declares"): the run plays the named plan's blocks in its order; its start row names the plan,
    whose blocks its task's content hash holds, and records no trial count of its own; its end row
    says how each block ended."""
    session = _planned(tmp_path, f'{{"short": Plan({_TWO}), "other": Plan({_TWO}[::-1])}}',
                       plan="short")

    session.run()

    start, end = _runs(session)
    assert (start["plan"], start["trials"], start["blocks"]) == ("short", None, ["near", "far"])
    assert session.blocks_run == ["near", "far"]
    assert session.stopped_because == "every block is finished"
    assert [(e["block"], e["ended"], e["by"]) for e in end["block_ends"]] == [
        ("near", "rule", None), ("far", "rule", None)]
    assert {row["block"] for row in _trial_rows(session)} == {"near", "far"}


def test_a_condition_setting_a_polar_position_plays_it_and_its_rows_record_it_polar_first(
        tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-20, "Both, polar first", through a plan: a condition may set a setting that
    holds a whole position to one of its `Polar` choices (since true visual angle merged); its
    trials play it, and each row records it as its eccentricity and direction with its (x, y)
    beside them."""
    from wl_xcon.geometry import polar_deg

    path = _sessions.polar_task(tmp_path)
    path.write_text(path.read_text() + (
        "\nfrom wl_xcon.task import Block, Condition, Plan\n"
        "TASK = _polar_dataclasses.replace(TASK, plans={'places': Plan([Block('b', [\n"
        "    Condition('right', {'target_pos': Polar(8.0, 0.0)}, target=2),\n"
        "    Condition('oblique', {'target_pos': Polar(8.0, 45.0)}, target=2)])])})\n"))
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, task=str(path), plan="places", trials=400))

    session.run()

    x, y = polar_deg(8.0, 45.0)
    assert {row["condition"]: row["params"]["target_pos"] for row in _trial_rows(session)} == {
        "right": {"eccentricity": 8.0, "direction": 0.0, "x": 8.0, "y": 0.0},
        "oblique": {"eccentricity": 8.0, "direction": 45.0, "x": x, "y": y}}
    assert session.stopped_because == "every block is finished"


def test_a_plain_run_records_no_plan_and_its_trial_count(tmp_path):
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, trials=3))

    session.run()

    start, end = _runs(session)
    assert (start["plan"], start["plan_form"], start["trials"], start["blocks"]) == (
        None, None, 3, None)
    assert end["block_ends"] == [{"block": "session", "ended": "rule", "by": None,
                                  "after_trial": 2, "block_in_session": 1, "position": 0,
                                  "passed_at": None}]
    assert end["end_reason"] == "plan_done"


def test_a_cyclic_plan_runs_until_its_time_limit_and_ends_as_designed_not_as_the_welfare_stop(
        tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Yes, in the plan": the run ends at the first pause between trials after
    its time; neither it nor a block's limit is a welfare limit, so its stop is `completed`, never
    `limit`, the out-of-cage welfare stop's kind. What the open block still owed is recorded as not
    run, "time ran out"."""
    session = _planned(tmp_path, f'{{"loop": Plan({_TWO}, order=Cyclic(), time_limit_s=30.0)}}',
                       plan="loop")

    session.run()

    (_, end) = _runs(session)
    assert session.stop_kind == "completed"
    assert session.stopped_because == "the plan's time limit is reached: 30 s of trials"
    assert session.blocks_run[:4] == ["near", "far", "near", "far"]
    assert end["block_ends"][-1]["ended"] == "run_time" and end["end_reason"] == "plan_time"
    owed = [s for s in end["shortfalls"] if s["because"] in ("time", "run_time")]
    assert all(s["block"] == session.blocks_run[-1] and s["because"] == "run_time" for s in owed)
    # Read between trials: the run overran its limit by no more than the trial in flight.
    assert 30.0 <= session.now() < 30.0 + 2 * LONGEST_TRIAL_S


def test_a_blocks_time_limit_ends_it_between_trials_and_records_what_it_owed_as_not_run(tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Finish trial, then end": the trial in flight finishes and pays if
    earned; the block ends at the next pause between trials; trials it still owed are recorded as
    not run, "time ran out"; a fixed plan then plays its next block."""
    session = _planned(tmp_path, (
        '{"timed": Plan([Block("long", [Condition("near", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=500)], '
        'time_limit_s=10.0), Block("far", [Condition("far", {"target_x": 12.0}, target=2)])])}'),
        plan="timed")

    session.run()

    (_, end) = _runs(session)
    assert session.blocks_run == ["long", "far"]
    assert [e["ended"] for e in end["block_ends"]] == ["time", "rule"]
    (short,) = [s for s in end["shortfalls"] if s["because"] == "time"]
    assert (short["block"], short["condition"]) == ("long", "near") and short["trials"] > 0
    assert not short["possible_avoidance"]


def test_time_paused_does_not_count_against_a_plans_time_limit(tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Pauses don't count": the clock runs only while trials run, so a pause
    longer than the limit leaves the run its minutes of work, and the welfare-listed paused loop
    (`Session._hold`) reads no time limit."""
    link = _Scripted(script={12: [Resume(by=Box("jake"))]}, step=5.0)
    path = _sessions.planned_task(tmp_path, f'{{"loop": Plan({_TWO}, order=Cyclic(), '
                                            f'time_limit_s=20.0)}}')
    session, wall = _walled(tmp_path, link, task=str(path), plan="loop", trials=400)
    link.wall = wall
    link.queue(Pause(by=Box("jake")))

    session.run()

    assert len(link.waits) == 12, "paused for 60 s of wall, three times the limit"
    assert session.stopped_because == "the plan's time limit is reached: 20 s of trials"
    assert len(_trial_rows(session)) > 0


def test_a_plan_that_can_draw_no_further_trial_ends_its_run_with_a_sentence(tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "End run with a sentence": "The run ends cleanly, saying why in plain
    words; the session stays open so the operator can start another run at once. Recorded as the
    run's end, not a crash." A cyclic plan whose every block owes nothing would advance forever;
    the per-advance progress check ends it. Its passes are bounded (`_Scripted`, Ruling 10), so
    without the check the run faults rather than spinning the suite."""
    session = _planned(tmp_path, (
        '{"empty": Plan([Block("a", [Condition("x", {}, target=0)]), '
        'Block("b", [Condition("y", {}, target=0)])], order=Cyclic(), time_limit_s=60.0)}'),
        plan="empty", link=_Scripted())

    session.run()

    (_, end) = _runs(session)
    assert session.stop_kind == "completed"
    assert session.stopped_because == (
        "the plan can draw no further trial: since the run's last trial each of its blocks (a, b) "
        "ended before it drew one, so the run ends here")
    assert end["trials"] == 0 and session.blocks_run == ["a", "b"]
    assert [e["ended"] for e in end["block_ends"]] == ["rule", "run"]
    assert end["end_reason"] == "stalled"


def test_a_block_that_cannot_draw_and_does_not_end_ends_its_run_with_a_sentence(tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "End run with a sentence": a block that can draw nothing and whose rule
    is not met -- here a criterion over no conditions, given in code past the load-time check that
    refuses it in a task (XC-381) -- ends the run saying why, where `next_trial` faulted it."""
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, trials=400,
                             blocks=[Block(name="empty", conditions=[], criterion=(0.8, 5))]))

    session.run()

    assert session.stop_kind == "completed"
    assert session.stopped_because == (
        "block empty has no condition left to draw and its end rule is not met, so the run ends "
        "here")
    assert [e["ended"] for e in _runs(session)[1]["block_ends"]] == ["run"]
    assert _runs(session)[1]["end_reason"] == "cannot_draw"


def test_a_training_progression_moves_up_falls_back_and_keeps_its_top_stage_in_a_run(
        tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19 ("Same, but top keeps going"), through a run: two right answers pass
    stage one; three wrong at stage two fall back to stage one, started fresh; two right pass it
    again; at the top the animal keeps working until the run's time limit."""
    from wl_xcon import taskd

    real, calls = taskd.run_trial, []
    script = [Outcome.CORRECT] * 2 + [Outcome.WRONG_TARGET] * 3 + [Outcome.CORRECT] * 200

    def scripted(*args, **kwargs):
        calls.append(None)
        return dataclasses.replace(real(*args, **kwargs), outcome=script[len(calls) - 1])

    monkeypatch.setattr(taskd, "run_trial", scripted)
    session = _planned(tmp_path, (
        '{"train": Plan([Block("one", [Condition("near", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=1)], '
        'criterion=(1.0, 2)), Block("two", [Condition("far", {"target_x": 12.0}, target=1)], '
        'criterion=(1.0, 2))], order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 3)), time_limit_s=40.0)}'),
        plan="train")

    session.run()

    (_, end) = _runs(session)
    assert session.blocks_run == ["one", "two", "one", "two"]
    assert [e["ended"] for e in end["block_ends"]] == ["rule", "fall_back", "rule", "run_time"]
    assert session.stopped_because == "the plan's time limit is reached: 40 s of trials"
    # The science review, I1: the top stage's rule was met after trial 8, and it worked on.
    assert end["block_ends"][-1]["passed_at"] == 8 and end["block_ends"][-1]["after_trial"] > 8
    # I2: each move to another block is said on the changes feed, with its numbers.
    assert [said for kind, _, _, said in session.controls if kind == "block"] == [
        "one → two: its rule met: 2 right in a row, every target used up",
        "two → one: fell back: 0 of its last 3 first answers right, under 50%",
        "one → two: its rule met: 2 right in a row, every target used up",
    ]


def _scripted_trials(monkeypatch, outcomes, frames: int | None = None) -> None:
    """Every trial `run_trial` runs ends with the next of `outcomes`, and lasts `frames` frames
    when given -- so a run's trial clock reads exactly frames at the frame period and the ITI."""
    from wl_xcon import taskd

    real, calls = taskd.run_trial, []

    def scripted(*args, **kwargs):
        calls.append(None)
        result = dataclasses.replace(real(*args, **kwargs), outcome=outcomes[len(calls) - 1])
        return result if frames is None else dataclasses.replace(result, frames=frames)

    monkeypatch.setattr(taskd, "run_trial", scripted)


def test_a_runs_time_limit_finishes_a_drummed_trial_first(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Finish the drum first", for a run's time limit: "Same as a block's
    limit: 'Training ends on a success.'" A drummed trial still coming back when the run's time is
    up comes back until it is right; the run ends at the boundary after it, its last line that
    drummed trial, right."""
    _scripted_trials(monkeypatch, [Outcome.WRONG_TARGET] * 30 + [Outcome.CORRECT] * 100)
    session = _planned(tmp_path, (
        '{"drill": Plan([Block("drill", [Condition("near", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=500)], '
        'drum=True)], time_limit_s=5.0)}'), plan="drill")

    session.run()

    rows = _trial_rows(session)
    assert len(rows) == 31, "thirty tries of one trial, the time up after the first ten"
    assert (rows[-1]["outcome"], rows[-1]["drummed"]) == ("correct", True)
    assert session.stopped_because == "the plan's time limit is reached: 5 s of trials"
    assert session._elapsed - session._run_from >= 15.0


def test_a_rule_met_as_the_runs_time_runs_out_closes_its_block_as_met(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """The science review, I3: a stage passed on the run's last trial is recorded as passed
    (`rule`), not as the run's time; a block the run's time ends unmet is the run's time's
    (`run_time`), apart from a block's own (`time`). Each trial lasts 1 s and its 0.5 s ITI, so
    the run's 7.4 s are up after the fifth -- the trial that meets the rule."""
    _scripted_trials(monkeypatch, [Outcome.WRONG_TARGET] * 2 + [Outcome.CORRECT] * 100,
                     frames=240)
    session = _planned(tmp_path, (
        '{"two": Plan([Block("a", [Condition("near", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=1)], '
        'criterion=(1.0, 3)), Block("b", [Condition("far", {"target_x": 12.0}, target=1)])], '
        'time_limit_s=7.4)}'), plan="two")

    session.run()

    (_, end) = _runs(session)
    assert len(_trial_rows(session)) == 5
    assert [(e["block"], e["ended"]) for e in end["block_ends"]] == [("a", "rule")]
    assert end["end_reason"] == "plan_time"


def test_each_block_end_names_its_block_in_the_stream_and_its_place_in_the_run(tmp_path):
    """The engineering review, I4: `block_ends` joins the stream's blocks by `block_in_session`,
    the number each block's `BLOCK_START` carried, null for a block that ran no trial and so was
    never opened in the stream; `position` is its place in the run's sequence."""
    session = _planned(tmp_path, (
        '{"three": Plan([Block("a", [Condition("x", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=2)]), '
        'Block("b", [Condition("y", {"target_x": 8.0}, target=0)]), '
        'Block("c", [Condition("z", {"target_x": 12.0}, target=2)])])}'), plan="three")

    session.run()

    (_, end) = _runs(session)
    assert [(e["block"], e["block_in_session"], e["position"]) for e in end["block_ends"]] == [
        ("a", 1, 0), ("b", None, 1), ("c", 2, 2)]
    opened = [word[1] for word in _block_words(session.card.codes)
              if isinstance(word, tuple) and word[0] == "start"]
    assert opened == [e["block_in_session"] for e in end["block_ends"]
                      if e["block_in_session"] is not None]
    assert {row["block"]: row["block_in_session"] for row in _trial_rows(session)} == {
        "a": 1, "c": 2}


def test_the_start_row_carries_the_plan_as_declared(tmp_path):
    """The science review, I4: the start row names the plan and writes it as declared, in the form
    the content hash reads it, so the record tells a balanced plan from a random one without the
    task file; `blocks` is the plan's block types as declared."""
    path = _sessions.planned_task(tmp_path, f'{{"mixed": Plan({_TWO}, order=Balanced(blocks=4))}}')
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, task=str(path), plan="mixed", trials=400))

    session.run()

    (start, _) = _runs(session)
    plan = _load_task(path).plans["mixed"]
    assert start["plan_form"] == json.loads(json.dumps(canonical(plan)))
    assert start["plan_form"]["fields"]["order"] == {"class": "Balanced",
                                                     "fields": {"blocks": 4}}
    assert start["blocks"] == ["near", "far"]


def test_a_time_limit_is_said_in_whole_minutes_and_seconds():
    """The engineering review, Minor 4: "1 minutes" and "3.33333 minutes" were said."""
    assert [_minutes(s) for s in (45, 60, 200, 3600)] == [
        "45 s", "1 minute", "3 minutes 20 s", "60 minutes"]


def test_a_run_naming_a_plan_its_task_lacks_is_refused_before_it_starts(tmp_path):
    """The backstop for a caller that took no pre-flight, as for a blocking finding."""
    session = _planned(tmp_path, f'{{"short": Plan({_TWO})}}', plan="long")

    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="the task declares no plan long; its plans are short"):
        session.run()

    assert not (session.directory / "runs.jsonl").exists()
```

```python
def _minutes(seconds: float) -> str:
    """A time limit in the words a sentence gives it: whole minutes and seconds, or seconds under
    a minute -- "1 minute", "3 minutes 20 s", "45 s" (the engine C2b plan's engineering review,
    Minor 4)."""
    if seconds < 60:
        return f"{seconds:g} s"
    minutes, rest = divmod(round(seconds), 60)
    return f"{minutes} minute{'' if minutes == 1 else 's'}" + (f" {rest} s" if rest else "")


#: **Why a run ended `completed`**, on its end row beside `stop_kind` (`end_reason`; engine build
#: C2b, the plan's science review, I3), for a reader that must not parse the sentence: its plan's
#: last block ended (`plan_done`), the plan's time limit (`plan_time`), the progress check -- every
#: block type opened since its last trial ended before it drew one (`stalled`) -- or a block that
#: can draw nothing and does not end (`cannot_draw`). `None` for a run that ended any other way.
END_REASONS = ("plan_done", "plan_time", "stalled", "cannot_draw")
```

```python
                            f"the plan can draw no further trial: since the run's last trial each "
                            f"of its blocks ({names}) ended before it drew one, so the run ends "
                            f"here"
                        )
                        self.stop_kind, end_reason = "completed", "stalled"
                        publish()
                        break
```

`docs/design/architecture.md`: replace

```markdown
in a plain "run N trials" run (every live run until C2b), whose table (`scheduler.EVERY_TRIAL`)
spends every non-answer, and said on the console. A hold runs its own time: the load-time check warns where a timeout on its
state can cut it short, a timeout listed before the hold that ties it included (`hold-cut-short`).

A session is a sequence of **blocks** (condition set, parameter overrides, length rule,
transition) and **interludes** (sub-tasks such as calibration that the session enters and
leaves without ending). Token economies require session-scoped state and a persistent
display layer that per-trial scenes do not reset.
```

with

```markdown
in a plain "run N trials" run, whose table (`scheduler.EVERY_TRIAL`)
spends every non-answer, and said on the console. A hold runs its own time: the load-time check warns where a timeout on its
state can cut it short, a timeout listed before the hold that ties it included (`hold-cut-short`).

**Plans and blocks, since engine build C2b.** A task declares its plans (`Task.plans`; engine spec
§13.1), and a run plays the one it is started with -- `wlx run --plan` -- or with none the plain
run.
A plan is its block types (`task.Block`: a named set of conditions, each naming the trial
structure it plays and counted by that structure's table unless its block has its own; ending once
every target is used up and, as declared, a criterion is met, a number of trials is run, or its
time runs out), how they follow (`Fixed`, `Cyclic`, `Balanced` -- shuffled rounds within the run --
`Random`, or `ByCriterion`, a training progression that moves up on a stage's rule or its time,
back one on a fall-back rule, and keeps its top stage going) and a run time limit. A block is one
stretch of trials under one type inside one run, opened by `BLOCK_START` before its first trial
and closed by `BLOCK_END` after its last. **Time limits are read between trials, in the run loop,
never in `Session._ends`**: on the time trials ran, pauses not counting; a drummed trial waiting
finishes first; neither is a welfare limit, and a run they end is `completed`, never `limit`, its
end row saying why (`end_reason`). A plan that can draw no further trial ends its run with a
sentence (the per-advance progress check).
A live edit wins over a value a condition sets (engine spec §14.7). The load-time checks refuse a
plan that cannot run (`bad-plan`) and warn of one that may not do what was meant. Each run's start
row names its plan and writes it as declared, its end row says how each block ended and which block
of the stream it was (`block_ends`), and each trial's row names the structure it played and any
value a live edit overrode.

A session is a sequence of runs, each a sequence of **blocks**, and **interludes** (sub-tasks
such as calibration that the session enters and leaves without ending; engine build C6). Token
economies require session-scoped state and a persistent display layer that per-trial scenes do
not reset.
```

```markdown
- **Basis:** PI decision, with published precedent. [@asaad2008flexible] (Results, "Design" and "Usability: Configuration menu") — blocks can switch on a learning criterion, "detecting when a subject has learned and only then switching blocks", and performance can be judged by a moving average over the last n trials against a threshold; [@nimhmonkeylogic2026task] — software documentation: a block-change function "is called after every trial to determine whether a new block should be initiated" ("Blocks"); [@berger2018standardized] — standardized, automated macaque training through successively more complex task levels, with task conditions updated automatically from each animal's progress (abstract). The 80%-over-20 example and the interlude rule have no published source.
```

`docs/references/decisions.md`: after


add

```markdown
### TSK-59 — A block's and a run's time limits count the time trials run, pauses not counting, and are read between trials: the trial in flight finishes and pays, a drummed trial waiting is answered right first, and what a block still owed is recorded as not run, "time ran out"; neither is a welfare limit, and a run so ended is completed
- **Decided:** 2026-10-19, the PI, answering the engine C2b scope's questions: "Finish trial, then end" -- "The trial finishes and pays if earned; the block ends at the next pause between trials. Trials it still owed are recorded as not run, 'time ran out'. It overruns by one trial at most."; "Finish the drum first" (time running out while a drummed trial comes back); "Yes, in the plan" -- a run's time limit "written in the task's plan, shown in the review report; the run ends at the first pause between trials after its time. The console's scheduled stop stays as it is; whichever comes first ends the run."; "Pauses don't count" -- "The block's clock runs only while trials run, so a limit means minutes of work."; and, answering the plan's science review, "Finish the drum first" for a run's limit too -- "Same as a block's limit: 'Training ends on a success.'" That the clock is the trial clock (each trial's frames at the nominal frame period and its ITI, unmeasured on a rig, XC-406), that a block the run's time ends is recorded as the run's (`run_time`) unless its rule is met at that boundary, and the stop kind `completed` with its `end_reason` are engineering ([the engine C2b plan](../superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-engine-c2b.md#calls), calls 6 to 8).
- **Where:** [engine spec §13](../superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-engine-design.md#13-structure-n10-nr1-nr6) and its note of YYYY-MM-DD; [`wl_xcon/taskd.py`](../../wl_xcon/taskd.py) (`Session.run`); [`wl_xcon/scheduler.py`](../../wl_xcon/scheduler.py) (`Scheduler.expire`, `ends`, `close`, `TIME`).
- **Basis:** PI decision; no published source. The out-of-cage limit (WEL-01) is the one welfare duration limit and is unchanged: a plan's time limit is design, never welfare.

### TSK-60 — A plan that can draw no further trial ends its run with a sentence saying why, recorded as the run's end, not a crash; the session stays open
- **Decided:** 2026-10-19, the PI: "End run with a sentence" -- "The run ends cleanly, saying why in plain words; the session stays open so the operator can start another run at once. Recorded as the run's end, not a crash." The check -- every block type opened since the last trial, or a person's last move, ended before it drew one, or a block that cannot draw does not end -- replacing `taskd`'s count of advances, and the stop kind `completed` with its `end_reason` (`stalled`, `cannot_draw`), are engineering ([the engine C2b plan](../superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-engine-c2b.md#calls), call 7).
- **Where:** [engine spec §13](../superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-engine-design.md#13-structure-n10-nr1-nr6) item 5 ("a per-advance progress check"); [`wl_xcon/scheduler.py`](../../wl_xcon/scheduler.py) (`Scheduler.stalled`, `drawable`); [`wl_xcon/taskd.py`](../../wl_xcon/taskd.py) (`Session.run`).
- **Basis:** PI decision; engineering for the check. No published source.

### TSK-61 — Condition numbers wait for the lab's condition registry: a trial's condition is recorded by its name until then
- **Decided:** 2026-10-19, the PI: "Wait for the registry" -- "Names only until C5; numbers that change meaning between task versions inside recordings are worse than none." Supersedes, for now, wl-preproc's acceptance of task-assigned numbers (its ruling of 2026-10-11; XC-197).
- **Where:** [engine spec §14](../superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-engine-design.md#14-variation-n11-nr1-nr2) item 5; backlog XC-197.
- **Basis:** PI decision; no published source.

```

```markdown
*Engine build C2b, YYYY-MM-DD* (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-engine-c2b.md`; the PI's
answers of 2026-10-19): **items 1, 5 and 8 built** -- a task's plans in its task object, chosen at
run start (`wlx run --plan`); block orders fixed, cyclic, balanced in shuffled rounds within each
run ("Shuffled rounds") and plain random ("Both, as two choices"), each random order drawn from the
run's recorded seed, a balanced round never starting with the type just played ("Never
back-to-back"), and by criterion: up on a stage's own rule, back one stage, started fresh, on the
plan's fall-back rule, judged once its window of answers is in and on each trial's first answer
only ("First answer only"; a broken hold where the stage scores one, "Yes, the same"), the top
stage working on ("Same, but top keeps going"), and a stage whose time runs out moving on whether
or not it passed ("Move on anyway"), the top stage's own time limit ending the run. A block ends on
every target
used up and, as declared, a criterion, a number of trials (a trial and its drummed repeats one) or
a time limit. **Time limits** -- a block's and the run's, the run's written in the plan ("Yes, in
the plan") -- are read between trials on the time trials ran, pauses not counting ("Pauses don't
count"): the trial in flight finishes and pays ("Finish trial, then end"), a drummed trial waiting
is answered right first ("Finish the drum first", for both), and what a block still owed is
recorded as not run, "time ran out", the run's time apart from a block's own; neither is a welfare
limit, and a run they end is `completed`, never the out-of-cage stop's `limit`, its end row saying
why (`end_reason`). **The per-advance progress check**: a plan whose every block ended before it
drew a trial since the last one, or a block that cannot draw and does not end, ends the run with a
sentence ("End run with a sentence"), recorded as the run's end, not a crash. Item 5's citation of
[@berger2018standardized] beside "fall-back", and §15's beside progressions that "may step back",
rest on automated progression through levels from each animal's progress, which its abstract
states; that it steps back is UNVERIFIED (TSK-57). Interludes (item 4) are build C6's; the
operator's edits (item 2), C3's.
```

---

### Task 8: A plan chosen at run start, over the link: the pre-flight, `Service._start` and telemetry schema 17

**The operator chooses at run start among the plans a task declares** (decision 18): `CheckRun.plan` and `StartRun.plan`, optional, `None` the plain run, decoded by the wire's own rules (call 12; `link._setting` untouched); `wlx serve`'s page bodies take them. **The plan names reach the page on the pre-flight** (`Preflight.plans`, the plans its task declares, and `Preflight.plan`, the one it was taken for), which loads the task already -- never by loading every task file on each housekeeping pass (Surprise 2). **The pre-flight is taken for the plan** (`Service._preflight(..., plan)`, call 18): a plan the task lacks fails its task checks item, so `preflight.gate` refuses the start unchanged and `_start` gains no branch (call 11); the starting values cover the structures the plan's conditions play, a number a condition sets given (Surprise 9); each condition's values over the starting values are re-checked widened (TSK-51), a warning naming the condition, or what would break the run failing the starting values. **`Service._start`, welfare-listed, changes in three lines** (Appendix A, item 1): its pre-flight taken for `command.plan`, `plan=command.plan` into the `RunSpec`, and its docstring's sentence ("until either exists (XC-207) a run is one block") made true. Its diff is printed by Task 12's comparison, and is short enough to review on its own: the task's reviewer is given `git diff HEAD~1 -- wl_xcon/service.py` beside the whole (the engineering review, Minor 6). **Telemetry schema 17** (call 14): `Telemetry.plan`, `next_block`, `block_left_s`, `run_left_s`, and -- for the console's words on a block past its targets, a passed top stage and the fall-backs (the science review, I1 and I2; Task 10) -- `trials_to_run`, `passed_at` and `fell_back`, read from the session (`Session.plan`, `Session.left`) and the scheduler, recomputing nothing; `Preflight.plan` and `plans`. A console built for 16 refuses 17 by name. The path is tested, not the pieces (call 20): a page start naming a plan through `Service._start`, its pre-flight and `Session.run`, to the end row. The backlog extends XC-387 to schema 17.

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/link.py`, `wl_xcon/serve.py`, `wl_xcon/service.py` (`_start` welfare-listed), `wl_xcon/preflight.py`, `wl_xcon/taskd.py`
- Modify (docs): `docs/design/architecture.md` (schema 17; `_start`'s paragraph; the plans paragraph), `docs/references/decisions.md` (TSK-59's `Session.left`), `docs/backlog.md` (XC-387)
- Modify (test): `tests/_frames.py`, `tests/test_cli.py` (the full frame), `tests/test_link.py`, `tests/test_preflight.py`, `tests/test_serve.py`, `tests/test_service.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RunSpec.plan`, `Session._played`, `_run_from`, `_block_from` (Task 7); `Scheduler.next_block`, `passed_at`, `fell_back` (Task 5).
- Produces: `link.SCHEMA = 17`; `CheckRun.plan`, `StartRun.plan: str | None = None`; `Preflight.plan: str | None = None`, `Preflight.plans: tuple = ()`; `Telemetry.plan`, `next_block`, `block_left_s`, `run_left_s`, `trials_to_run`, `passed_at`, `fell_back`; `Session.plan` (property), `Session.left(which) -> float | None`; `preflight.task(..., plan=None)`, `beyond(..., plan=None)`, `values(..., plan=None)`; `Service._preflight(session, task, values, plan=None)`; `serve._SHAPES["check"|"start"]` take `plan`.

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Schema 17 (engine build C2b): the plan a run plays, its next block and its time left
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_schema_17_carries_the_plan_the_next_block_the_time_left_and_the_plans_offered():
    """Engine build C2b: the plan the run plays, the block it goes on to, the seconds of trials
    left before the block's and the run's time limits, and a pre-flight's plan and the plans its
    task offers. A reader of 17 refuses 16 by name."""
    preflight = Preflight(task="planned.py", items=(PreflightItem("task checks", "pass", "ok"),),
                          plan="train", plans=("train", "test"))
    sent = _telemetry(plan="train", next_block="hard", block_left_s=12.5, run_left_s=600.0,
                      trials_to_run=200, passed_at=41, fell_back=2, preflight=preflight)

    assert decode(encode(sent)) == sent
    assert decode(encode(replace(sent, plan=None, next_block=None, block_left_s=None,
                                 run_left_s=None, preflight=replace(preflight, plan=None,
                                                                    plans=())))).plan is None
    with pytest.raises(SchemaMismatch, match="carried schema 16 and this console reads schema 17"):
        decode(encode(replace(sent, schema=16)))


def test_a_check_and_a_start_carry_the_plan_by_its_name_or_none_for_the_plain_run():
    """Engine build C2b: decoded by the wire's own rules, the plan is text of 1 to `TEXT_LIMIT`
    characters, or absent for the plain run; refused otherwise, naming the field."""
    check = CheckRun(by=Box("jake"), task="planned.py", values={}, plan="train")
    start = StartRun(by=Box("jake"), task="planned.py", values={}, trials=5, acknowledged=(),
                     plan="train")
    plain = StartRun(by=Box("jake"), task="planned.py", values={}, trials=5, acknowledged=())

    for command in (check, start, plain):
        assert _decode_command(_encode_command(command)) == command
    assert plain.plan is None
    for wrong in ("", 7, "x" * (TEXT_LIMIT + 1)):
        with pytest.raises(CommandRefused, match="a 'start' command's plan is text"):
            _command_from({"kind": "start", "by": {"kind": "box", "name": "jake"},
                           "task": "planned.py", "values": {}, "trials": 5, "acknowledged": [],
                           "plan": wrong})


def test_a_frame_names_the_runs_plan_its_next_block_and_its_time_left(tmp_path):
    """`Telemetry.of` reads the session's plan and time left (`Session.plan`, `Session.left`) and
    the scheduler's next block, recomputing nothing."""
    session = SimpleNamespace(**{**vars(_session_with(1.25, 3.0)), "plan": "train",
                                 "left": lambda which: {"block": 3.0, "run": 30.0}[which]})
    scheduler = Scheduler([Block("easy", [Condition("a", {}, target=1)]),
                           Block("hard", [Condition("b", {}, target=1)])], seed=0)

    frame = Telemetry.of(session, Tally(), scheduler, 0)

    assert (frame.plan, frame.next_block, frame.block_left_s, frame.run_left_s) == (
        "train", "hard", 3.0, 30.0)
    assert (frame.trials_to_run, frame.passed_at, frame.fell_back) == (None, None, 0)
    assert Telemetry.of(session, None, None, 0).block_left_s is None


def test_a_frame_says_when_a_top_stage_passed_how_often_the_plan_fell_back_and_its_trials():
    """The engine C2b plan's science review, I1 and I2: the trial after which a training
    progression's top stage first met its rule, the times the plan fell back a stage, and the open
    block's number of trials, read from the scheduler."""
    session = SimpleNamespace(**{**vars(_session_with(1.25, 3.0)), "plan": "train",
                                 "left": lambda which: None})
    stages = [Block("easy", [Condition("a", {}, target=1)], criterion=(1.0, 1)),
              Block("hard", [Condition("b", {}, target=1)], criterion=(1.0, 1), trials=5)]
    scheduler = Scheduler(stages, seed=0, order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 1)))
    for outcome in (Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, Outcome.CORRECT):
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, outcome)
        if scheduler.ends is not None:
            scheduler.advance(scheduler.ends)
    for _ in range(5):
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.ends is None

    frame = Telemetry.of(session, Tally(), scheduler, 0)

    assert (frame.trials_to_run, frame.passed_at, frame.fell_back) == (5, 7, 1)


```

```python
def test_a_run_of_a_plan_needs_values_for_the_structures_its_conditions_play_only():
    """Engine build C2b: a plan's run plays the structures its conditions name, so a number only
    another structure uses needs no starting value; and a number every condition playing its
    structure sets is given by the plan."""
    trial = _trial()
    cued = dataclasses.replace(
        trial,
        params=[*trial.params, Param("cue_delay", unit="s", low=0.0, high=1.0)],
        windows=[dataclasses.replace(trial.windows[0], radius=P("cue_delay")), *trial.windows[1:]],
    )
    plain = Plan([Block("b", [Condition("c", {}, target=1)])])
    cueing = Plan([Block("b", [Condition("c", {"cue_delay": 0.2}, target=1, structure="cued"),
                               Condition("d", {}, target=1)])])
    half = Plan([Block("b", [Condition("c", {"cue_delay": 0.2}, target=1, structure="cued"),
                             Condition("d", {}, target=1, structure="cued")])])
    task = Task({"detection": trial, "cued": cued}, default="detection",
                plans={"plain": plain, "cueing": cueing, "half": half})

    assert preflight.values(task, {}, plan="plain").result == "pass"
    assert preflight.values(task, {}, plan="cueing").result == "pass"
    item = preflight.values(task, {}, plan="half")
    assert item.result == "fail" and "'cue_delay' is used by the task" in item.said


def test_a_plan_its_task_does_not_declare_fails_the_task_checks(tmp_path):
    """Engine build C2b: so `gate` refuses a start naming one, `Service._start` unchanged in
    that."""
    path = _sessions.planned_task(tmp_path, '{"short": Plan([Block("b", [Condition("c", {}, '
                                            'target=1)])])}')

    named, _, _ = preflight.task(path, ALLOCATION, DIRECT, SRGB, plan="short")
    missing, _, _ = preflight.task(path, ALLOCATION, DIRECT, SRGB, plan="long")

    assert named.result == "pass"
    assert missing.result == "fail"
    assert missing.said == "the task declares no plan 'long'; its plans are 'short'"


def test_each_conditions_values_are_held_to_their_ranges_as_starting_values_are(tmp_path):
    """Engine build C2b, under the PI's rule of 2026-10-11 (XC-352's D2): a value a condition sets
    outside its range is a warning naming the condition, and one that would break the run -- a
    target off the screen -- fails the starting values, naming it too."""
    path = _sessions.planned_task(tmp_path, (
        '{"wide": Plan([Block("b", [Condition("near", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=1), '
        'Condition("past", {"target_x": 17.0}, target=1)])]), '
        '"off": Plan([Block("b", [Condition("away", {"target_x": 16.0, "target_y": 40.0}, '
        'target=1)])])}'))
    task = _load_task(path)

    wide = preflight.beyond(task, {}, ALLOCATION, DIRECT, SRGB, None, plan="wide")
    off = preflight.beyond(task, {}, ALLOCATION, DIRECT, SRGB, None, plan="off")

    assert [f.code for f in wide] == ["outside-range"]
    assert wide[0].detail.startswith("in plan 'wide', block 'b', condition 'past': 'target_x'")
    assert not wide[0].blocking
    refused = [f for f in off if f.blocking]
    assert refused and all("condition 'away'" in f.detail for f in refused)
    assert preflight.values(task, {}, refused, plan="off").result == "fail"
    assert preflight.beyond(task, {}, ALLOCATION, DIRECT, SRGB, None) == []

```

```python

# --- A plan chosen at run start (engine build C2b) ---------------------------------------------

#: A plan of two blocks of `fixation_detection`'s, near then far, each owed two trials.
_PLANS = ('{"short": Plan([Block("near", [Condition("near", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=2)]), '
          'Block("far", [Condition("far", {"target_x": 12.0}, target=2)])]), '
          '"timed": Plan([Block("long", [Condition("near", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=500)])], '
          'time_limit_s=10.0)}')


def test_a_check_is_taken_for_the_plan_it_names_and_lists_the_plans_its_task_offers(tmp_path):
    """Engine build C2b: the plan names reach the page on the pre-flight, which loads the task --
    never by loading every task file on each pass (`Service._tasks`)."""
    service = _service(tmp_path)
    planned_task(service.tasks, _PLANS)
    _step(service, _open())

    planned = _step(service, CheckRun(by=BY, task="planned.py", values=dict(VALUES), plan="short"))
    plain = _step(service, CheckRun(by=BY, task="planned.py", values=dict(VALUES)))
    other = _step(service, CheckRun(by=BY, task=TASK, values=dict(VALUES)))

    assert (planned.preflight.plan, planned.preflight.plans) == ("short", ("short", "timed"))
    assert (plain.preflight.plan, plain.preflight.plans) == (None, ("short", "timed"))
    assert (other.preflight.plan, other.preflight.plans) == (None, ())


def test_a_start_from_the_page_plays_its_plan_through_to_the_runs_end_row(tmp_path):
    """Test the path, not the piece (PIT P21): a page's start naming a plan, through
    `Service._start` -- the welfare-listed function whose one new field this is -- its own
    pre-flight and `Session.run`, to the start row naming the plan and the end row saying how each
    block ended."""
    service = _service(tmp_path)
    planned_task(service.tasks, _PLANS)
    _step(service, _open())

    frame = _step(service, _start(task="planned.py", plan="short"))

    start, end = _runs(service.root)
    assert (start["plan"], start["trials"], start["blocks"]) == ("short", None, ["near", "far"])
    assert [(e["block"], e["ended"]) for e in end["block_ends"]] == [("near", "rule"),
                                                                     ("far", "rule")]
    assert (frame.phase, frame.stop_kind, frame.plan) == ("between_runs", "completed", "short")


def test_a_start_naming_a_plan_its_task_lacks_is_refused_by_its_pre_flight(tmp_path):
    """The pre-flight's task checks item fails, so `preflight.gate` refuses the start unchanged and
    `Service._start` gains no branch of its own (the plan's call 11)."""
    service = _service(tmp_path)
    planned_task(service.tasks, _PLANS)
    _step(service, _open())

    frame = _step(service, _start(task="planned.py", plan="long"))

    assert frame.run_index is None and _runs(service.root) == []
    assert ("pre-flight failed, so the run does not start: task checks: the task declares no plan "
            "'long'; its plans are 'short', 'timed'") in _refused(frame)[-1]


def test_a_timed_plan_ends_its_run_as_designed_and_the_session_stays_open(tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19: a plan's time limit is no welfare limit; the run ends `completed` with
    its sentence, and the session is back between runs, ready for another."""
    service = _service(tmp_path)
    planned_task(service.tasks, _PLANS)
    _step(service, _open())

    frame = _step(service, _start(task="planned.py", plan="timed"))

    assert (frame.phase, frame.stop_kind) == ("between_runs", "completed")
    assert frame.stopped_because == "the plan's time limit is reached: 10 s of trials"
    again = _step(service, _start())
    assert again.run_index == 1 and again.stop_kind == "completed"


```

```python
    #: The plan the run plays, by its name in the task, or `None` for the plain run (schema 17;
    #: engine build C2b).
    plan: str | None
    #: `scheduler.next_block`'s name (schema 17): the block the plan goes on to as the open one
    #: ends on its rule or its time -- a training progression's next stage up -- or `None` where
    #: the plan ends there.
    next_block: str | None
    #: The seconds of trials left before the open block's time limit and the run's (schema 17),
    #: on the time trials ran, a pause not counting (the PI, 2026-10-19); `None` with no limit.
```
