```python
    #: `scheduler.passed_at` (schema 17): the trial after which a training progression's top stage

    passed_at: int | None
    #: `scheduler.fell_back` (schema 17): how many times the run's plan fell back a stage (the
```

`wl_xcon/preflight.py`: replace

```python
    with any value, and no value need be outside its range (Task 10's review, M3)."""
    if task is None:

    try:

```

with

```python
    with any value, and no value need be outside its range (Task 10's review, M3). **And each
    condition of the run's plan**, its values over the starting ones (engine build C2b): a value
    a condition sets is held to its range as a starting value is, each finding naming it."""
    if task is None:
        return []
    try:
        start = starting(task, given)
        found = beyond_range(task, start, allocation, geometry=geometry,
                             calibration=calibration, deployment=deployment)
        if plan is None or plan not in task.plans:
            return found
        for block in task.plans[plan].blocks:
            for condition in block.conditions:
                for finding in beyond_range(task, {**start, **condition.values}, allocation,
                                            geometry=geometry, calibration=calibration,
                                            deployment=deployment):
                    if finding in found:
                        continue
                    found.append(dataclasses.replace(finding, detail=(
                        f"in plan {plan!r}, block {block.name!r}, condition "
                        f"{condition.name!r}: {finding.detail}")))
        return found
```

```python
        # Every structure's numbers, since engine build C1: a run's values cover each trial
        # structure its task can play, failing closed (the engine C1 plan's call 15); build C2's
```

with

```python
        # The numbers of every structure the run can play: a plain run's, every structure's,
        # failing closed (the engine C1 plan's call 15); a plan's, the structures its conditions
        # play (engine build C2b), less what each condition playing it sets.
        conditions = ([c for block in task.plans[plan].blocks for c in block.conditions]
                      if plan is not None and plan in task.plans else None)
        if conditions is None:
            used = frozenset().union(*(parameters_used(s) for s in task.structures.values()))
        else:
            used = frozenset(
                name for c in conditions
                for name in parameters_used(task.structures[c.structure or task.default])
                if name not in c.values
            )
```

`wl_xcon/taskd.py`: after

```python
        """One frame of this session as it stands, for a caller between its runs."""
        self._publish()

```

add

```python
    @property
    def plan(self) -> str | None:
        """The plan the run in progress, or the last one, plays, by its name in its task; `None`
        for the plain run, or before any run (engine build C2b)."""
        return None if self._run is None else self._run.plan

    def left(self, which: str) -> float | None:
        """The seconds of trials left before the open block's time limit (`"block"`) or the run's
        (`"run"`), on the time trials ran, a pause not counting (the PI, 2026-10-19); `None` with
        no limit, or before any run (engine build C2b): what the console shows."""
        scheduler, plan = self._scheduler, self._played
        if scheduler is None or plan is None:
            return None
        if which == "block":
            limit, since = scheduler.block.time_limit_s, self._block_from
        else:
            limit, since = plan.time_limit_s, self._run_from
        return None if limit is None else max(0.0, limit - (self._elapsed - since))

```

`docs/design/architecture.md`: replace

```markdown
| `console` | The control box, in a browser on the LAN | Python server + web client | Experimenter UI, live plots, parameter writes, preflight, test screens. **The box authenticates and records the actor** — anybody attached has full access, with visibility rather than a lock (S9a §8); `wl-works` lists devices and links to them, and carries no welfare-affecting action (ADR-0008). **The link exists** (`wl_xcon/link.py`, P4d-1, 2026-09-19): `taskd` holds a `Link` port, drained and published once per trial boundary and never per frame, whose live implementation (`ZmqLink`) binds a ZMQ PUB socket for `Telemetry`, a REP socket for its commands — `SetParameter` and `Stop`, and since P4d-2b b2a `Pause`, `Resume`, `Mark` (a mark's note), `ScheduleStop`, `CancelScheduledStop` and `ManualReward` (a manual reward: while paused and, in a `wlx taskd` session, between runs and while the return is awaited), and since P4d-2b b3a `OpenSession`, `CheckRun`, `StartRun` and `EndSession`, and since XC-026 (2026-10-01) `ResumeSession` (wire kind `resume_session`: a stranded session resumed from its record), which `wlx taskd` acts on and a `wlx run` session refuses — which the page sends since b3a-2 — from its forms, and since XC-026 from the stranded banner's *resume session* button, and since engine build C2a, from its Still-needed pane, `ClearOwed` (`clear`: one trial, or the rest, cleared from a condition's count), `Drum` (`drum`: drumming switched for a block or a run) and `Training` (`training`: the training aid *clear*, or the setting that judges only each drummed trial's first answer for a percentage, switched on or off) — through `POST /commands`, built by the wire's own rules (`link._command_from`), and whose closed session's summary `wlx taskd`'s idle frame carries until the next session opens (telemetry schema 16, `link.SCHEMA`; the summary since 11) — each checked where it is decoded — and, given a third endpoint, a PULL socket for an operator's mark signal, which the trial loop checks once per frame (ADR-0003's transport, untouched: a third socket on the same link). `ZmqConsole` is the other end. Reached today by `wlx run --link PUB,REP` or `wlx taskd --link PUB,REP` and a terminal client, `wlx console --sub PUB --req REP --as WHO`. **The browser console exists** (P4d-2b slice b1, 2026-09-26, read-only then; the box's writes since b2a and its session forms since b3a-2, below): `wlx serve --link PUB,REP --http HOST:PORT --health-token-file PATH` is its own process — a stdlib `ThreadingHTTPServer`, one `ZmqConsole` on a telemetry thread, server-sent events to each browser from a bounded queue, and every pane rendered in Python (`web.py`) so the page's script only swaps fragments. The wl-works fonts are bundled and served by the box, so the page never reaches the internet (PI, 2026-09-26). Reads are open to the LAN. **Writes come from the box** (P4d-2b slice b2a, 2026-09-28): `POST /commands` is accepted only from a loopback peer, with a `Host` naming loopback, the page's own `Origin` and `Content-Type: application/json` (spec §2), and recorded as the box's actor map (`{"kind": "box", "name": …}`) and shown as `NAME (box, unverified)`; every request is answered only when its `Host` names this console (`--allow-host` adds names). `wlx serve` owns each socket on one thread — a read-only telemetry thread, a command thread whose REQ socket waits for `taskd`'s acknowledgment (*sent*, *not delivered*, *busy*), and a mark thread that sends the signal ahead of every command. Writes from people signed in to wl-works are b2b slice 2, below | Runs against a fake `taskd` (`link.Simulated`), or a real one over loopback sockets |
```

Run the five files: `1069 passed`. Then the whole suite: **4117 passed**.

- [ ] **Step 6: Prove they can fail**

- `wl_xcon/preflight.py`: `unknown = [] if plan is None or plan in loaded.plans else [` → `unknown = [] if True else [` -- `tests/test_preflight.py::test_a_plan_its_task_does_not_declare_fails_the_task_checks` fails: `1 failed`.
- `wl_xcon/preflight.py`: `if conditions is None:` → `if True:` -- `tests/test_preflight.py::test_a_run_of_a_plan_needs_values_for_the_structures_its_conditions_play_only` fails: `1 failed`.
- `wl_xcon/preflight.py`: `if plan is None or plan not in task.plans:` → `if True:` -- `tests/test_preflight.py::test_each_conditions_values_are_held_to_their_ranges_as_starting_values_are` fails: `1 failed`.
- `wl_xcon/service.py`: delete `plan=command.plan,` -- `tests/test_service.py::test_a_start_from_the_page_plays_its_plan_through_to_the_runs_end_row` fails: `1 failed`.
- `wl_xcon/service.py`: `checked, owed = self._preflight(session, task, command.values, command.plan)` → `checked, owed = self._preflight(session, task, command.values)` -- `tests/test_service.py::test_a_start_naming_a_plan_its_task_lacks_is_refused_by_its_pre_flight` fails: `1 failed`.
- `wl_xcon/link.py`: `plan = _word(data, "plan", kind, by, optional=True)` → `plan = data.get("plan")` -- `tests/test_link.py::test_a_check_and_a_start_carry_the_plan_by_its_name_or_none_for_the_plain_run` fails: `1 failed`.

---

### Task 9: *Next block* and *back one*, by hand

The PI's answer Q10, "'Next' and 'back one'": "Both directions by hand, each recorded with who; two new commands." **`link.NextBlock`** (`next_block`) and **`link.BackBlock`** (`back_block`), fieldless as the pause is, decoded and encoded by the wire's own rules and built from the page's bodies (`serve._SHAPES`). **Each passes through `Session._command` as a branch beside the pause** -- two `isinstance` branches after C2a's three, handing to `Session._move`, outside the list (Appendix A, item 2): `_command`'s welfare-listed parts, its `held` pass-through and its one `except` line, unchanged, and Task 12 compares the rest. `_move` asks `Scheduler.move` (Task 5): the run moves at once, a drummed trial waiting dropped -- **and named in the move's row** (`dropped`; the science review, M5); the block left closes in the stream (`BLOCK_END`, where it ran a trial); the block's time starts afresh; `controls.jsonl` records who moved it from which block to which, **its `event_code` null** (the engineering review, C1: wl-preproc times the n-th row naming a code by the n-th strobe of that code, and every block that ends on its own strobes `BLOCK_END` with no row, so counts would never agree; the row is timed by its own instant, the block's end by the stream), and the end row's `block_ends` says `next` or `back` with who. **A move back before a block's first trial no longer ends a run as stalled** (Task 5's fix, here through a run: the engineering review's I1, its fuzz's seed 167). Refused with the sentence, nothing changed, in a plain run (one block), past the plan's last block and before its first. Because `_hold` drains through `_command`, a move sent while paused is applied while paused (Review Focus 4). The docs record the commands (architecture.md's command list and its welfare paragraph; the engine spec §13's note; TSK-62).

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/link.py`, `wl_xcon/serve.py`, `wl_xcon/taskd.py` (`_command`: two branches beside the pause; `_move`)
- Modify (docs): `docs/design/architecture.md`, the engine spec (§13's note), `docs/references/decisions.md` (TSK-62)
- Modify (test): `tests/test_link.py`, `tests/test_serve.py`, `tests/test_taskd.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Scheduler.move`, `drum_waiting`, `NEXT`, `BACK` (Task 5); `Session._block_from` (Task 7).
- Produces: `link.NextBlock(by)`, `link.BackBlock(by)` (in `Command`); `Session._move(command, index, step)`; `controls.jsonl` rows `next_block` and `back_block` (`block`, `to`, `dropped`, `event_code` null).

```python
# --- A person moves the run on a block, or back one (engine build C2b) ---------------------

#: Three block types of one condition each, every condition owed four trials.
_THREE = ('{"three": Plan([Block("a", [Condition("x", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=4)]), '
          'Block("b", [Condition("y", {"target_x": 8.0}, target=4)]), '
          'Block("c", [Condition("z", {"target_x": 12.0}, target=4)])])}')


def test_a_person_moves_a_run_on_a_block_and_back_one_and_each_is_recorded_with_who(tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "'Next' and 'back one'": "Both directions by hand, each recorded with
    who". *Next* leaves `a` after its first trial; *back one*, after two trials of `b`, plays `a`
    again, started fresh; the stream closes each block as it is left, and the controls and the end
    row say who."""
    link = _Drained({2: [NextBlock(by=Box("kim"))], 4: [BackBlock(by=Box("kim"))]})
    session = _planned(tmp_path, _THREE, plan="three", link=link)

    session.run()

    (_, end) = _runs(session)
    assert session.blocks_run == ["a", "b", "a", "b", "c"]
    kim = {"kind": "box", "name": "kim"}
    assert [(e["block"], e["ended"], e["by"]) for e in end["block_ends"]] == [
        ("a", "next", kim), ("b", "back", kim), ("a", "rule", None), ("b", "rule", None),
        ("c", "rule", None)]
    moves = [(row["kind"], row["block"], row["to"], row["event_code"])
             for row in _controls_rows(session) if row["kind"] in ("next_block", "back_block")]
    # The engineering review, C1: a move's row names no code -- every block that ends on its own
    # strobes `BLOCK_END` with no row, so wl-preproc could not time the n-th row naming 3 by the
    # n-th 3 -- and it is timed by its own instant; the stream still closes each block left.
    assert moves == [("next_block", "a", "b", None), ("back_block", "b", "a", None)]
    rows = _trial_rows(session)
    assert [row["block"] for row in rows[:4]] == ["a", "b", "b", "a"]
    assert sum(row["block"] == "a" for row in rows) == 5, "one before next, four once back"
    assert _block_words(session.card.codes) == [
        ("run", 1), ("start", 1), "end", ("start", 2), "end", ("start", 3), "end", ("start", 4),
        "end", ("start", 5), "end", "run end"]


def test_a_move_back_before_a_blocks_first_trial_does_not_end_its_run_as_stalled(tmp_path):
    """The engineering review, I1 (its fuzz's seed 167): a fixed plan whose first block owes
    nothing and whose second owes three; a person moves back one before the second's first trial,
    the first ends on its rule again, and the second plays its three -- where the run ended with
    "the plan can draw no further trial", false for the second."""
    link = _Drained({2: [BackBlock(by=Box("kim"))]})
    session = _planned(tmp_path, (
        '{"two": Plan([Block("a", [Condition("x", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=0)]), '
        'Block("b", [Condition("y", {"target_x": 12.0}, target=3)])])}'), plan="two", link=link)

    session.run()

    (_, end) = _runs(session)
    assert session.blocks_run == ["a", "b", "a", "b"]
    assert [e["ended"] for e in end["block_ends"]] == ["rule", "back", "rule", "rule"]
    assert len(_trial_rows(session)) == 3 and end["end_reason"] == "plan_done"


def test_a_move_that_drops_a_drummed_trial_says_so_in_its_row(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """The science review, M5: a person's move acts at once, dropping a drummed trial waiting to
    come back (the engineering call; Appendix B), and its row says which."""
    _scripted_trials(monkeypatch, [Outcome.WRONG_TARGET] + [Outcome.CORRECT] * 50)
    link = _Drained({2: [NextBlock(by=Box("kim"))]})
    session = _planned(tmp_path, (
        '{"drill": Plan([Block("a", [Condition("x", {"target_x": 5.0}, target=4)], drum=True), '
        'Block("b", [Condition("y", {"target_x": 12.0}, target=2)])])}'), plan="drill", link=link)

    session.run()

    (row,) = [row for row in _controls_rows(session) if row["kind"] == "next_block"]
    assert (row["block"], row["to"], row["dropped"]) == ("a", "b", "x")
    (said,) = [said for kind, _, _, said in session.controls if kind == "next_block"]
    assert said == "next block: from block a to block b; the drummed trial of x waiting was dropped"


def test_a_move_with_no_block_to_go_to_is_refused_with_its_sentence_and_changes_nothing(tmp_path):
    """A plain run is one block; a plan's first block has none before it."""
    plain = _session(_spec(tmp_path / "plain", trials=3), link=_Drained(
        {1: [NextBlock(by=Box("kim"))]}))
    planned = _planned(tmp_path / "planned", _THREE, plan="three", link=_Drained(
        {1: [BackBlock(by=Box("kim"))]}))

    plain.run()
    planned.run()

    assert [why for _, _, why in plain.refusals] == [
        "a plain run is one block, so there is no block to move to; a run of one of the task's "
        "plans moves between its blocks"]
    assert [why for _, _, why in planned.refusals] == [
        "block 'a' is the first of the run's blocks, so there is no block before it"]
    assert planned.blocks_run == ["a", "b", "c"]


def test_a_move_sent_while_paused_is_applied_at_once(tmp_path):
    """As a clear or a drumming switch is: `_hold` drains it into `Session._command`, beside the
    pause, outside its welfare-listed parts, so the run resumes in the block moved to."""
    link = _Scripted(script={1: [NextBlock(by=Box("kim"))], 2: [Resume(by=Box("kim"))]})
    path = _sessions.planned_task(tmp_path, _THREE)
    session, _ = _walled(tmp_path, link, task=str(path), plan="three", trials=400)
    link.queue(Pause(by=Box("kim")))

    session.run()

    kinds = [row["kind"] for row in _controls_rows(session)]
    assert kinds[:3] == ["pause", "next_block", "resume"]
    assert _trial_rows(session)[0]["block"] == "b"
    assert session.blocks_run == ["a", "b", "c"]


```

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class NextBlock:
    """**Move the run on to its next block** (engine build C2b; the PI, 2026-10-19, "'Next' and
    'back one'": "Both directions by hand, each recorded with who"): the block its plan's order
    gives next, a training progression's next stage up, at the boundary the command reaches, or
    at once while paused (`scheduler.Scheduler.move`)."""

    KIND: ClassVar[str] = "next_block"

    by: Actor


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BackBlock:
    """**Move the run back one block** (engine build C2b; the PI, 2026-10-19): the block before the
    open one in the run's sequence, a training progression's stage below, started fresh."""

    KIND: ClassVar[str] = "back_block"

    by: Actor


```

add

```python
    def _move(self, command, index: int, step: str) -> None:
        """**A person moves the run on to its next block, or back one** (`link.NextBlock`,
        `link.BackBlock`; engine build C2b; the PI, 2026-10-19, "'Next' and 'back one'": "Both
        directions by hand, each recorded with who"), between trials or while paused: the scheduler
        moves (`Scheduler.move`), the open block closes in the stream (`BLOCK_END`, where it ran a
        trial) as an advance's does, its time starts afresh, and `controls.jsonl` records who moved
        it from which block to which, and a drummed trial waiting that the move dropped (the plan's
        science review, M5), the run's end row how the block ended. **The row names no event
        code** (the engineering review, C1): wl-preproc times the n-th row naming a code by the
        n-th time that code was strobed, and every block that ends on its own strobes `BLOCK_END`
        with no row, so a move's row is timed by its own instant. **Refused with the sentence**,
        nothing changed, where there is no such block: a plain run is one block, and the first or
        last block of a plan has none before or after it."""
        scheduler = self._scheduler
        if self._run is not None and self._run.plan is None and not self._run.blocks:
            self._refuse(command.KIND, command.by, (
                "a plain run is one block, so there is no block to move to; a run of one of the "
                "task's plans moves between its blocks"))
            return
        was, dropped = scheduler.block.name, scheduler.drum_waiting
        said = scheduler.move(step, command.by)
        if said is not None:
            self._refuse(command.KIND, command.by, said)
            return
        if self._levels.end_block():
            self.card.emit(BLOCK_END)
        self.blocks_run.append(scheduler.block.name)
        self._block_from = self._elapsed
        what = "next block" if step == NEXT else "back one"
        self._control(
            command.KIND, command.by,
            f"{what}: from block {was} to block {scheduler.block.name}"
            + ("" if dropped is None else f"; the drummed trial of {dropped} waiting was dropped"),
            index, block=was, to=scheduler.block.name, dropped=dropped,
        )

```

```markdown
| `console` | The control box, in a browser on the LAN | Python server + web client | Experimenter UI, live plots, parameter writes, preflight, test screens. **The box authenticates and records the actor** — anybody attached has full access, with visibility rather than a lock (S9a §8); `wl-works` lists devices and links to them, and carries no welfare-affecting action (ADR-0008). **The link exists** (`wl_xcon/link.py`, P4d-1, 2026-09-19): `taskd` holds a `Link` port, drained and published once per trial boundary and never per frame, whose live implementation (`ZmqLink`) binds a ZMQ PUB socket for `Telemetry`, a REP socket for its commands — `SetParameter` and `Stop`, and since P4d-2b b2a `Pause`, `Resume`, `Mark` (a mark's note), `ScheduleStop`, `CancelScheduledStop` and `ManualReward` (a manual reward: while paused and, in a `wlx taskd` session, between runs and while the return is awaited), and since P4d-2b b3a `OpenSession`, `CheckRun`, `StartRun` and `EndSession`, and since XC-026 (2026-10-01) `ResumeSession` (wire kind `resume_session`: a stranded session resumed from its record), which `wlx taskd` acts on and a `wlx run` session refuses — which the page sends since b3a-2 — from its forms, and since XC-026 from the stranded banner's *resume session* button, and since engine build C2a, from its Still-needed pane, `ClearOwed` (`clear`: one trial, or the rest, cleared from a condition's count), `Drum` (`drum`: drumming switched for a block or a run) and `Training` (`training`: the training aid *clear*, or the setting that judges only each drummed trial's first answer for a percentage, switched on or off) — through `POST /commands`, built by the wire's own rules (`link._command_from`), and whose closed session's summary `wlx taskd`'s idle frame carries until the next session opens (telemetry schema 17, `link.SCHEMA`; the summary since 11) — each checked where it is decoded — and, given a third endpoint, a PULL socket for an operator's mark signal, which the trial loop checks once per frame (ADR-0003's transport, untouched: a third socket on the same link). `ZmqConsole` is the other end. Reached today by `wlx run --link PUB,REP` or `wlx taskd --link PUB,REP` and a terminal client, `wlx console --sub PUB --req REP --as WHO`. **The browser console exists** (P4d-2b slice b1, 2026-09-26, read-only then; the box's writes since b2a and its session forms since b3a-2, below): `wlx serve --link PUB,REP --http HOST:PORT --health-token-file PATH` is its own process — a stdlib `ThreadingHTTPServer`, one `ZmqConsole` on a telemetry thread, server-sent events to each browser from a bounded queue, and every pane rendered in Python (`web.py`) so the page's script only swaps fragments. The wl-works fonts are bundled and served by the box, so the page never reaches the internet (PI, 2026-09-26). Reads are open to the LAN. **Writes come from the box** (P4d-2b slice b2a, 2026-09-28): `POST /commands` is accepted only from a loopback peer, with a `Host` naming loopback, the page's own `Origin` and `Content-Type: application/json` (spec §2), and recorded as the box's actor map (`{"kind": "box", "name": …}`) and shown as `NAME (box, unverified)`; every request is answered only when its `Host` names this console (`--allow-host` adds names). `wlx serve` owns each socket on one thread — a read-only telemetry thread, a command thread whose REQ socket waits for `taskd`'s acknowledgment (*sent*, *not delivered*, *busy*), and a mark thread that sends the signal ahead of every command. Writes from people signed in to wl-works are b2b slice 2, below | Runs against a fake `taskd` (`link.Simulated`), or a real one over loopback sockets |

`wl-works` lists devices and links to them, and carries no welfare-affecting action (ADR-0008). **The link exists** (`wl_xcon/link.py`, P4d-1, 2026-09-19): `taskd` holds a `Link` port, drained and published once per trial boundary and never per frame, whose live implementation (`ZmqLink`) binds a ZMQ PUB socket for `Telemetry`, a REP socket for its commands — `SetParameter` and `Stop`, and since P4d-2b b2a `Pause`, `Resume`, `Mark` (a mark's note), `ScheduleStop`, `CancelScheduledStop` and `ManualReward` (a manual reward: while paused and, in a `wlx taskd` session, between runs and while the return is awaited), and since P4d-2b b3a `OpenSession`, `CheckRun`, `StartRun` and `EndSession`, and since XC-026 (2026-10-01) `ResumeSession` (wire kind `resume_session`: a stranded session resumed from its record), which `wlx taskd` acts on and a `wlx run` session refuses — which the page sends since b3a-2 — from its forms, and since XC-026 from the stranded banner's *resume session* button, and since engine build C2a, from its Still-needed pane, `ClearOwed` (`clear`: one trial, or the rest, cleared from a condition's count), `Drum` (`drum`: drumming switched for a block or a run) and `Training` (`training`: the training aid *clear*, or the setting that judges only each drummed trial's first answer for a percentage, switched on or off), and since engine build C2b `NextBlock` (`next_block`) and `BackBlock` (`back_block`: the run moved on a block, or back one, by hand) — through `POST /commands`, built by the wire's own rules (`link._command_from`), and whose closed session's summary `wlx taskd`'s idle frame carries until the next session opens (telemetry schema 17, `link.SCHEMA`; the summary since 11) — each checked where it is decoded — and, given a third endpoint, a PULL socket for an operator's mark signal, which the trial loop checks once per frame (ADR-0003's transport, untouched: a third socket on the same link). `ZmqConsole` is the other end. Reached today by `wlx run --link PUB,REP` or `wlx taskd --link PUB,REP` and a terminal client, `wlx console --sub PUB --req REP --as WHO`. **The browser console exists** (P4d-2b slice b1, 2026-09-26, read-only then; the box's writes since b2a and its session forms since b3a-2, below): `wlx serve --link PUB,REP --http HOST:PORT --health-token-file PATH` is its own process — a stdlib `ThreadingHTTPServer`, one `ZmqConsole` on a telemetry thread, server-sent events to each browser from a bounded queue, and every pane rendered in Python (`web.py`) so the page's script only swaps fragments. The wl-works fonts are bundled and served by the box, so the page never reaches the internet (PI, 2026-09-26). Reads are open to the LAN. **Writes come from the box** (P4d-2b slice b2a, 2026-09-28): `POST /commands` is accepted only from a loopback peer, with a `Host` naming loopback, the page's own `Origin` and `Content-Type: application/json` (spec §2), and recorded as the box's actor map (`{"kind": "box", "name": …}`) and shown as `NAME (box, unverified)`; every request is answered only when its `Host` names this console (`--allow-host` adds names). `wlx serve` owns each socket on one thread — a read-only telemetry thread, a command thread whose REQ socket waits for `taskd`'s acknowledgment (*sent*, *not delivered*, *busy*), and a mark thread that sends the signal ahead of every command. Writes from people signed in to wl-works are b2b slice 2, below | Runs against a fake `taskd` (`link.Simulated`), or a real one over loopback sockets |
```

### Task 10: The page: the plan picker, the plan's row, *next block* and *back one*

The PI's answer Q9, "Preselect first, keep plain": "The task's first plan is preselected; the plain run stays as one more labeled choice. One click to start; a wrong plan shows in the pre-flight and the run's start record." **The plan picker** (`plan-sel`, a static select beside `task-sel`, its options a fragment): the plans the shown pre-flight's task declares, in order, then the plain run (`PLAIN_RUN`), the plan the pre-flight was taken for selected, so the select always shows what the pre-flight is for -- a frame's choice stands there, unlike the task's. **The page learns a task's plans only from its pre-flight**, so choosing a task takes the plain run's pre-flight first; **the first time a task's plans arrive with a plain run's pre-flight, the script preselects the first and takes its pre-flight** (`preselect`, the options naming the task they are for, `data-for`, `plansOf` the task whose plans the page has offered) -- whether a person chose the task or it is the only one the rig offers, where the drafted script preselected only on a change of the task selector, so a one-task rig never did (the engineering review, I3); a choice made since, the plain run included, stands, and so does a pre-flight another member took for a plan. While a plan is chosen the trials box is hidden (call 12). *Start run* carries the pre-flight's plan (`data-plan`) and is sent only while it is the one chosen; the pre-flight's line names the plan or the plain run. **While a plan runs, the Still-needed pane leads with its row**: the plan, the block, the time left before the block's and the run's limits (a pause not counting), the block that comes next, and **next block** and **back one**, *next block* greyed at the plan's last block, saying why (the rig refuses *back one* at the first, saying so). What a block's time left owed reads "not run: its block's time ran out", and what the run's time left owed "not run: the run's time ran out" (the science review, I3). **A block past its targets says what it draws until** -- its criterion, its number of trials, or both -- where every block said "until the criterion is met", and **a passed top stage says "top stage passed at trial N; keeps working"**; the plan's row counts the fall-backs (the science review, I1 and I2). The plain run is labeled "plain run: the trials below, each counted once, none shown again" (its I8). `RUN_TRIALS`' comment is made true (call 24). Three browser tests drive the page's own script: choosing a task with plans preselects its first and *start run* sends it; on a rig offering one task, the pre-flight pill's plans preselect its first; the moves reach the rig as the member's. The docs record the picker (architecture.md; the engine spec §13's note; TSK-63).

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/web.py`
- Modify (docs): `docs/design/architecture.md`, the engine spec (§13's note), `docs/references/decisions.md` (TSK-63)
- Modify (test): `tests/test_web.py`, `tests/test_serve.py` (the end-to-end page test's start button), `tests/test_page_browser.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Preflight.plan`, `plans`, `Telemetry.plan`, `next_block`, `block_left_s`, `run_left_s`, `trials_to_run`, `passed_at`, `fell_back` (Task 8); `NextBlock`, `BackBlock` as the page's `next_block` and `back_block` (Task 9).
- Produces: `web.PLAIN_RUN`, `_plans(frame)`, `_plan_row(frame, view)`, `_left(seconds)`; `FRAGMENT_IDS` gains `plan-sel`; `_FORGIVEN["time"]` and `["run_time"]`; the start button's `data-plan`; the script's `chosenPlan`, `showTrials`, `preselect`, `plansOf`.

```python
# --- Plans (engine build C2b) ----------------------------------------------------------------


def _between_runs(**over):
    """A `wlx taskd` session between runs offering a task with plans, its first run ended."""
    fields = dict(phase="between_runs", service=True, run_index=0, stop_kind="completed",
                  stopped_because="every block is finished",
                  offered_tasks=("fixation_detection.py", "planned.py"))
    fields.update(over)
    return frame(**fields)


def _checks(rig) -> list:
    return [seen for seen in rig.dispatch.seen if isinstance(seen, CheckRun)]


def test_choosing_a_task_preselects_its_first_plan_and_start_sends_the_plan_its_preflight_is_for(
        rig):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Preselect first, keep plain": choosing a task takes its pre-flight,
    and once that brings the task's plans the page preselects the first, takes the pre-flight for
    it and hides the plain run's trials box; *start run* then sends that plan, as the member's.
    Test the path through the page's own script: the select, the frame, the swap, the post."""
    rig.hub.offer(_between_runs())
    page = rig.browser_page()
    _sign_in(page)
    page.select_option("#task-sel", "planned.py")
    rig.wait_for(lambda: _checks(rig))
    passing = (PreflightItem("task checks", "pass", "planned.py passes its load-time checks"),)
    rig.hub.offer(_between_runs(preflight=Preflight("planned.py", passing, plan=None,
                                                    plans=("train", "test"))))
    rig.wait_for(lambda: len(_checks(rig)) == 2)
    rig.hub.offer(_between_runs(preflight=Preflight("planned.py", passing, plan="train",
                                                    plans=("train", "test"))))
    page.wait_for_selector('[data-cmd="start"][data-plan="train"]:not([disabled])')
    hidden = page.evaluate('document.getElementById("run-trials").closest("label").hidden')
    page.click('[data-cmd="start"]')
    rig.wait_for(lambda: any(isinstance(seen, StartRun) for seen in rig.dispatch.seen))

    first, second = _checks(rig)
    started = next(seen for seen in rig.dispatch.seen if isinstance(seen, StartRun))
    assert (first.task, first.plan, second.task, second.plan) == (
        "planned.py", None, "planned.py", "train")
    assert hidden is True
    assert (started.task, started.plan, started.by.name) == (
        "planned.py", "train", "Jake Westerberg")


def test_the_only_task_offered_has_its_first_plan_preselected_as_its_plans_arrive(rig):
    """The engineering review, I3: on a rig offering one task, the task selector never changes, so
    the page preselected nothing. Its plans arrive with the pre-flight the pill takes, and the
    first is preselected then and its pre-flight taken -- once: the plan's own pre-flight leaves
    it standing."""
    rig.hub.offer(_between_runs(offered_tasks=("planned.py",)))
    page = rig.browser_page()
    _sign_in(page)
    page.click('[data-cmd="check"]')
    rig.wait_for(lambda: _checks(rig))
    passing = (PreflightItem("task checks", "pass", "planned.py passes its load-time checks"),)
    rig.hub.offer(_between_runs(offered_tasks=("planned.py",), preflight=Preflight(
        "planned.py", passing, plan=None, plans=("train", "test"))))
    rig.wait_for(lambda: len(_checks(rig)) == 2)
    rig.hub.offer(_between_runs(offered_tasks=("planned.py",), preflight=Preflight(
        "planned.py", passing, plan="train", plans=("train", "test"))))
    page.wait_for_selector('[data-cmd="start"][data-plan="train"]:not([disabled])')

    assert page.evaluate('document.getElementById("plan-sel").value') == "train"
    assert [(check.task, check.plan) for check in _checks(rig)] == [
        ("planned.py", None), ("planned.py", "train")]


def test_next_block_and_back_one_reach_the_rig_as_the_members(rig):
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "'Next' and 'back one'": "Both directions by hand, each recorded with
    who"."""
    rig.hub.offer(frame(plan="train", block="easy", next_block="hard"))
    page = rig.browser_page()
    _sign_in(page)

    page.click('[data-cmd="next_block"]')
    rig.wait_for(lambda: rig.dispatch.seen)
    page.click('[data-cmd="back_block"]')
    rig.wait_for(lambda: len(rig.dispatch.seen) == 2)

    moved, back = rig.dispatch.seen
    assert (type(moved), moved.by.name) == (NextBlock, "Jake Westerberg")
    assert (type(back), back.by.name) == (BackBlock, "Jake Westerberg")


```

```python
# --- Plans (engine build C2b) ----------------------------------------------------------------

PLANNED = Preflight(
    "planned.py",
    (PreflightItem("task checks", "pass", "planned.py passes its load-time checks"),),
    plan="train",
    plans=("train", "test"),
)


def test_the_plan_picker_offers_the_tasks_plans_then_the_plain_run_the_shown_preflights_chosen():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Preselect first, keep plain": the task's plans in order, then the plain
    run as one more labeled choice; the plan the shown pre-flight was taken for selected, so a wrong
    plan shows; no pre-flight, the plain run alone."""
    planned = fragments(_between(preflight=PLANNED), view())["plan-sel"]
    plain = fragments(_between(preflight=PREFLIGHT), view())["plan-sel"]

    assert planned == (
        '<option value="train" data-for="planned.py" selected>plan train</option>'
        '<option value="test" data-for="planned.py">plan test</option>'
        f'<option value="" data-for="planned.py">{PLAIN_RUN}</option>'
    )
    assert plain == (
        f'<option value="" data-for="fixation_detection.py" selected>{PLAIN_RUN}</option>')
    assert fragments(_between(), view())["plan-sel"] == f'<option value="">{PLAIN_RUN}</option>'
    # The science review, I8: "every trial paying" read as "every trial rewarded".
    assert PLAIN_RUN == "plain run: the trials below, each counted once, none shown again"
    assert fragments(idle(), view())["plan-sel"] == f'<option value="">{PLAIN_RUN}</option>'
    assert 'data-plan="train"' in fragments(_between(preflight=PLANNED), view())["controls"]
    assert "for planned.py · plan train · a fail blocks" in fragments(
        _between(preflight=PLANNED), view())["preflight"]


def test_a_tasks_first_plan_is_preselected_once_its_plans_arrive_and_a_plan_hides_the_trials():
    """The page learns a task's plans from its pre-flight, so choosing a task takes the plain run's
    first, then -- that task's plans arrived -- preselects its first plan and takes its pre-flight;
    a person's own choice of plan, the plain run included, stands."""
    preselect = _function("preselect")

    assert "if (task && task === chosenTask() && task !== plansOf) {" in preselect
    assert "if (first.value && !select.value && betweenRuns()) {" in preselect
    assert "select.value = first.value;" in preselect and "takePreflight(false);" in preselect
    assert 'if (id === "plan-sel") { preselect(); }' in _function("swap")
    assert ('if (betweenRuns()) { plansOf = null; el("plan-sel").value = ""; '
            "takePreflight(false); }") in _SCRIPT
    assert 'else if (e.target.id === "plan-sel") {\n      plansOf = chosenTask();' in _SCRIPT
    assert ('el("run-trials").closest("label").hidden = Boolean(chosenPlan());'
            in _function("showTrials"))


def test_a_running_plan_shows_its_block_what_comes_next_its_time_left_and_the_moves():
    """Engine build C2b: the plan, the open block, the next, the time left before the block's and
    the run's limits, and *next block* and *back one* (the PI, 2026-10-19), *next block* greyed at
    the plan's last block; a plain run shows none of it."""
    running = fragments(frame(plan="train", block="easy", next_block="hard", block_left_s=125.0,
                              run_left_s=1800.0), view())["rt-need"]
    last = fragments(frame(plan="train", block="hard", next_block=None), view())["rt-need"]

    assert ("plan train · block easy · 2:05 of the block&#x27;s time left · 30:00 of the "
            "run&#x27;s time left · next: hard") in running
    assert '<button type="button" class="btn" data-cmd="next_block">next block</button>' in running
    assert '<button type="button" class="btn" data-cmd="back_block">back one</button>' in running
    assert ('data-cmd="next_block" disabled title="this block is the last its plan gives"'
            in last and "the plan&#x27;s last block" in last)
    assert 'data-cmd="next_block"' not in fragments(frame(), view())["rt-need"]


def test_what_a_blocks_time_left_owed_is_said_as_not_run():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19: trials a block still owed when its time ran out are "recorded as not
    run, 'time ran out'"."""
    shown = fragments(frame(shortfalls=(ShortfallRow("easy", "near", 3, "time", False, None, 40),)),
                      view())["rt-need"]

    assert "3 short in easy · not run: its block's time ran out" in shown


def test_what_the_runs_time_left_owed_is_said_as_the_runs():
    """The plan's science review, I3: the console's label says whose time ran out."""
    shown = fragments(frame(shortfalls=(
        ShortfallRow("easy", "near", 3, "run_time", False, None, 40),)), view())["rt-need"]

    assert "3 short in easy · not run: the run's time ran out" in shown


def test_a_block_past_its_targets_says_what_it_draws_until_and_a_passed_top_stage_says_so():
    """The plan's science review, I1: "drawing until the criterion is met" was said of a block of
    a number of trials too, and a top stage passed said nothing; each now says what is true -- its
    criterion, its number of trials, or both -- and a training progression's passed top stage
    says when it passed and that the animal keeps working."""
    def need(**over) -> str:
        return fragments(frame(past_targets=True, **over), view())["rt-need"]

    assert "every target used up: drawing until the criterion is met<" in need(rule="percentage")
    assert "every target used up: drawing until 200 trials are run<" in need(trials_to_run=200)
    assert ("drawing until the criterion is met and 200 trials are run" in
            need(rule="streak", trials_to_run=200))
    passed = fragments(frame(plan="train", block="hard", passed_at=41), view())["rt-need"]
    assert "top stage passed at trial 41; keeps working" in passed
    assert "drawing until" not in passed


def test_the_plan_row_counts_how_often_the_plan_fell_back():
    """The plan's science review, I2: a move-back count in the plan row."""
    def row(fell_back) -> str:
        return fragments(frame(plan="train", block="easy", fell_back=fell_back), view())["rt-need"]

    assert "fell back" not in row(0)
    assert "the plan&#x27;s last block · fell back once" in row(1)
    assert " · fell back 3 times" in row(3)


```

```python
def _plans(frame: Telemetry | Idle | None) -> str:
    """The plan picker's options (engine build C2b; the PI, 2026-10-19, "Preselect first, keep
    plain"): the plans the shown pre-flight's task declares, in order, then the plain run, one
    more labeled choice; the plan the pre-flight was taken for selected, so a wrong one shows. Each
    names the task it is for (`data-for`): the page's script preselects a task's first plan once
    that task's own plans arrive with its pre-flight. No pre-flight shown, the plain run alone."""
    preflight = None if frame is None or isinstance(frame, Idle) else frame.preflight
    if preflight is None:
        return f'<option value="">{_e(PLAIN_RUN)}</option>'
    task = _e(preflight.task)

    def option(value: str, label: str) -> str:
        chosen = " selected" if (preflight.plan or "") == value else ""
        return f'<option value="{_e(value)}" data-for="{task}"{chosen}>{_e(label)}</option>'

    return "".join(option(name, f"plan {name}") for name in preflight.plans) + option("", PLAIN_RUN)


```

```python
}


def _left(seconds: float) -> str:
    """Seconds of trials left, as minutes and seconds."""
    whole = int(seconds)
    return f"{whole // 60}:{whole % 60:02d}"


def _plan_row(frame: Telemetry, view: View) -> str:
    """The run's plan (engine build C2b): its name, the open block and the one its plan goes on
    to, the time left before the block's and the run's limits, a pause not counting (the PI,
    2026-10-19), and *next block* and *back one* (the PI, 2026-10-19, "'Next' and 'back one'":
    "Both directions by hand, each recorded with who"), *next block* greyed, saying why, at the
    plan's last block. The rig refuses *back one* at the run's first block, saying so. And how
    often a training progression fell back a stage (the plan's science review, I2)."""
    said = [f"plan {frame.plan}", f"block {frame.block}"]
    if frame.block_left_s is not None:
        said.append(f"{_left(frame.block_left_s)} of the block's time left")
    if frame.run_left_s is not None:
        said.append(f"{_left(frame.run_left_s)} of the run's time left")
    said.append(f"next: {frame.next_block}" if frame.next_block is not None
                else "the plan's last block")
    if frame.fell_back:
        # How often the plan moved the animal back a stage (the plan's science review, I2).
        said.append("fell back once" if frame.fell_back == 1
                    else f"fell back {frame.fell_back} times")
    nothing = ("" if frame.next_block is not None
               else ' disabled title="this block is the last its plan gives"')
    return (
        f'<div class="kv"><span class="nm">{_e(" · ".join(said))}</span>'
        f'<button type="button" class="btn" data-cmd="next_block"{_gate(view, nothing)}>'
        f"next block</button>"
        f'<button type="button" class="btn" data-cmd="back_block"{_gate(view)}>back one</button>'
        f"</div>"
    )
```

```python
  // Engine build C2b (the PI, 2026-10-19, "Preselect first, keep plain"): the plan a run plays,
  // the empty choice the plain run; a task's first plan preselected once, as its plans first
  // arrive with a plain run's pre-flight -- the task chosen or the only one offered (the
  // engineering review, I3) -- a choice made since standing; the trials box hidden while a plan
  // is chosen. `plansOf`, the task whose plans the page has offered.
  var plansOf = null;
  function chosenPlan() { return el("plan-sel").value || null; }
  function showTrials() { el("run-trials").closest("label").hidden = Boolean(chosenPlan()); }
  function preselect() {
    var select = el("plan-sel");
    var first = select.options[0];
    var task = first ? first.getAttribute("data-for") : null;
    if (task && task === chosenTask() && task !== plansOf) {
      plansOf = task;
      if (first.value && !select.value && betweenRuns()) {
        select.value = first.value;
        takePreflight(false);
      }
    }
    showTrials();
  }
```

### Task 11: The plan in tables, in the review report

Engine spec §18.1: a task's review report carries "the plan in tables" (its census and timing diagram are C8's), and the PI's answer Q5 put a run's time limit "in the task's plan, shown in the review report". **`review._plans`**, after the counting tables under "What a reviewer confirms": each plan's order in words (`_order`: a balanced order never one type twice in a row, a fall-back on first answers -- the PI's answers 13 and 16) and its run time limit, a time said in whole minutes and seconds (`_seconds`; the engineering review, Minor 4); a condition's value that is a position -- a `Polar` or a pair, since true visual angle merged -- shown eccentricity and direction first, its (x, y) beside it, as the report shows every position (`_set_label`, `_point_label`; the PI, 2026-10-19, "Polar first, x-y allowed"; call 33); a row per block -- what ends it (its targets, a criterion in words, its number of trials, its time limit), how it draws its conditions, what it counts by, whether it drums; and a row per condition -- the structure it plays, its target, the values it sets. A task with none says the plain run is its only run. The default structure's heading, "its runs play it until plans exist (engine build C2)", is made true (call 24). The engine spec §18.1's note records it.

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/review.py`
- Modify (docs): the engine spec (§18.1's note)
- Modify (test): `tests/test_review.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `task.Plan` and its parts (Task 3).
- Produces: `review._plans(task) -> list[str]`, `_order`, `_block_order`, `_rule`, `_seconds`, `_set_label`; `render_task` shows them.

```python
def test_the_report_shows_each_plan_in_tables():
    """Engine spec §18.1, "the plan in tables" (engine build C2b): each plan's order and run time
    limit; each block's end rules, how it draws its conditions, what it counts by and whether it
    drums; each condition's structure, target and values. A task with none says so."""
    plans = {
        "train": Plan(
            [Block("easy", [Condition("near", {"contrast": 0.9}, target=10)],
                   criterion=(0.8, 20), time_limit_s=300.0, drum=True),
             Block("hard", [Condition("far", {"contrast": 0.2}, target=10, structure="other")],
                   criterion=(1.0, 5), trials=40, counting=Counting(after_stimulus=SPENT),
                   order=Constrained(max_run=2))],
            order=ByCriterion(fall_back=(0.5, 20)), time_limit_s=1800.0),
        "loop": Plan([Block("easy", [Condition("near", {}, target=4)])], order=Cyclic(cycles=3)),
    }
    out = render_task(_reviewed(plans=plans))

    assert "### Plans" in out and "#### Plan `train`" in out and "#### Plan `loop`" in out
    assert ("A training progression, stage by stage from the first: up one when a stage's rule is "
            "met or its time runs out; back one, started fresh, under 50% right over the last 20 "
            "first answers; the top stage keeps working until a time limit, a stop or the "
            "session's end. "
            "The run ends at the first pause between trials after 30 minutes of trials.") in out
    assert ("| easy | every target used up; 80% right over the last 20 answers; or its time runs "
            "out at 5 minutes | shuffled passes | each condition's structure's table | yes |"
            in out)
    assert ("| hard | every target used up; 5 right in a row; 40 trials run | drawn with "
            "replacement, runs of at most 2 | its own table | no |") in out
    assert "| train | " not in out
    assert "| easy | near | holding | 10 | contrast = 0.9 |" in out
    assert "| hard | far | other | 10 | contrast = 0.2 |" in out
    assert "In order, over and over: 3 passes. No run time limit." in out
    assert out.index("### Plans") < out.index("## Structure `holding`")
    assert "None: the plain run only" in render_task(_reviewed())


def test_a_conditions_position_is_shown_eccentricity_and_direction_first():
    """The PI, 2026-10-19, "Polar first, x-y allowed": a value a condition sets that is a position
    -- a whole-point setting's `Polar` or pair -- is shown in the plan's table as every position in
    the report is, eccentricity and direction first and its (x, y) beside them; a number as
    written."""
    from wl_xcon.task import Polar

    plans = {"places": Plan([Block("b", [
        Condition("oblique", {"pos": Polar(8.0, 45.0), "contrast": 0.5}, target=2),
        Condition("right", {"pos": (5.0, 0.0)}, target=2)])])}
    out = render_task(_reviewed(plans=plans))

    assert ("| b | oblique | holding | 2 | pos = 8° at 45° · (x, y) (5.675, 5.675), "
            "contrast = 0.5 |") in out
    assert "| b | right | holding | 2 | pos = 5° at 0° · (x, y) (5, 0) |" in out


def test_the_plan_tables_say_a_time_in_whole_minutes_and_a_balanced_order_never_back_to_back():
    """The engineering review, Minor 4 ("1 minutes", "3.33333 minutes"), and the PI's "Never
    back-to-back" (2026-10-19), as the report says them."""
    plans = {"mixed": Plan([Block("a", [Condition("near", {}, target=4)], time_limit_s=200.0),
                            Block("b", [Condition("far", {}, target=4)])],
                           order=Balanced(blocks=4), time_limit_s=60.0)}
    out = render_task(_reviewed(plans=plans))

    assert ("In shuffled rounds within the run, each block type once a round, drawn from the run's "
            "seed, never one type twice in a row: 4 blocks. The run ends at the first pause "
            "between trials after 1 minute of trials.") in out
    assert "or its time runs out at 3 minutes 20 s" in out
```

add

```python
def _rule(rule: tuple, answers: str = "answers") -> str:
    """A criterion or a fall-back rule in words: a proportion of 1.0 is a streak. A fall-back
    judges each trial's first answer only (the PI, 2026-10-19, "First answer only")."""
    proportion, window = rule
    if proportion >= 1.0:
        return f"{window} right in a row"
    return f"{100 * proportion:g}% right over the last {window} {answers}"


def _seconds(seconds: float) -> str:
    """A time in whole minutes and seconds, or seconds under a minute (the engine C2b plan's
    engineering review, Minor 4: "1 minutes", "3.33333 minutes")."""
    if seconds < 60:
        return f"{seconds:g} s"
    minutes, rest = divmod(round(seconds), 60)
    return f"{minutes} minute{'' if minutes == 1 else 's'}" + (f" {rest} s" if rest else "")


def _order(order) -> str:
    """How a plan's blocks follow one another, in words (engine spec §13.5)."""
    if isinstance(order, Cyclic):
        times = ("until a time limit, a stop or the out-of-cage limit" if order.cycles is None
                 else f"{order.cycles} passes")
        return f"In order, over and over: {times}."
    if isinstance(order, Balanced):
        count = ("round after round until a time limit, a stop or the out-of-cage limit"
                 if order.blocks is None else f"{order.blocks} blocks")
        return (f"In shuffled rounds within the run, each block type once a round, drawn from the "
                f"run's seed, never one type twice in a row: {count}.")
    if isinstance(order, Random):
        count = ("until a time limit, a stop or the out-of-cage limit" if order.blocks is None
                 else f"{order.blocks} blocks")
        return f"Each block a type drawn at random from the run's seed: {count}."
    if isinstance(order, ByCriterion):
        back = ("no fall-back" if order.fall_back is None
                else f"back one, started fresh, under {_rule(order.fall_back, 'first answers')}")
        return ("A training progression, stage by stage from the first: up one when a stage's "
                f"rule is met or its time runs out; {back}; the top stage keeps working until a "
                "time limit, a stop or the session's end.")
    return "Each block once, in order."


def _block_order(order) -> str:
    """How a block orders its conditions, in words."""
    if isinstance(order, Constrained):
        weights = f", weighted {order.weights}" if order.weights else ""
        return f"drawn with replacement, runs of at most {order.max_run}{weights}"
    if isinstance(order, WithReplacement):
        return "drawn with replacement" + (f", weighted {order.weights}" if order.weights else "")
    return "shuffled passes"


def _set_label(value: object) -> str:
    """A value a condition sets, as the plan's table shows it: **a position eccentricity and
    direction first, its (x, y) beside them**, as every position in this report is shown
    (`_point_label`; the PI, 2026-10-19, "Polar first, x-y allowed"), whether the setting takes a
    `Polar` or a pair; anything else as written."""
    from wl_xcon.task import Polar

    if isinstance(value, Polar) or (isinstance(value, tuple) and len(value) == 2):
        return _point_label(value)
    return f"{value}"


def _plans(task: Task) -> list[str]:
    """**The plan in tables** (engine spec §18.1; engine build C2b): each plan's order and run time
    limit, then a row for each block -- what ends it, how it orders its conditions, what it counts
    by, whether it drums -- and a row for each condition: the structure it plays, its target and
    the values it sets. A task with none says the plain run is its only run."""
    lines = ["### Plans", ""]
    if not task.plans:
        return lines + ["None: the plain run only -- one block of the default structure, every "
                        "trial paying.", ""]
    for name, plan in task.plans.items():
        limit = ("No run time limit." if plan.time_limit_s is None
                 else f"The run ends at the first pause between trials after "
                      f"{_seconds(plan.time_limit_s)} of trials.")
        lines += [f"#### Plan `{name}`", "", f"{_order(plan.order)} {limit}", "",
                  "| Block | Ends once | Conditions drawn | Counts by | Drums |",
                  "|---|---|---|---|---|"]
        for block in plan.blocks:
            ends = ["every target used up"]
            if block.criterion is not None:
                ends.append(_rule(block.criterion))
            if block.trials is not None:
                ends.append(f"{block.trials} trials run")
            if block.time_limit_s is not None:
                ends.append(f"or its time runs out at {_seconds(block.time_limit_s)}")
            counts = ("its own table" if block.counting is not None
                      else "each condition's structure's table")
            lines.append(f"| {block.name} | {_cell('; '.join(ends))} | "
                         f"{_cell(_block_order(block.order))} | {counts} | "
                         f"{'yes' if block.drum else 'no'} |")
        lines += ["", "| Block | Condition | Structure | Target | Values |",
                  "|---|---|---|---|---|"]
        for block in plan.blocks:
            for condition in block.conditions:
                values = ", ".join(f"{k} = {_set_label(v)}"
                                   for k, v in condition.values.items()) or "none"
                lines.append(f"| {block.name} | {condition.name} | "
                             f"{condition.structure or task.default} | {condition.target} | "
                             f"{_cell(values)} |")
        lines.append("")
    return lines


```

```bash
cd <worktree> && BASE=$(git merge-base HEAD main) && $PY - "$BASE" <<'EOF'
import ast
import difflib
import subprocess
import sys

BASE = sys.argv[1]
LISTED = {
    "wl_xcon/preflight.py": ["gate", "out_of_cage"],
    "wl_xcon/service.py": ["Service._open", "Service._end", "Service._unended",
                           "Service._close_stranded", "Service._start", "_unasked", "_folder_name"],
    "wl_xcon/taskd.py": ["OUTSIDE_A_RUN", "Session._ends", "Session._hold",
                         "Session._manual_reward", "Session.set", "Session._schedule"],
    "wl_xcon/link.py": ["_setting"],
    "wl_xcon/cli.py": ["_settle_departure", "_settle_return"],
}
#: C2a's three branches, approved (the engine C2a plan's Appendix A, items 1 and 4).
C2A = '''        if isinstance(command, _link.ClearOwed):
            self._clear(command, index)
            return
        if isinstance(command, _link.Drum):
            self._drum(command, index)
            return
        if isinstance(command, _link.Training):
            self._train(command, index)
            return
'''
#: C2b's two, beside them (the engine C2b plan's Appendix A, item 2).
C2B = '''        if isinstance(command, _link.NextBlock):
            self._move(command, index, NEXT)
            return
        if isinstance(command, _link.BackBlock):
            self._move(command, index, BACK)
            return
'''


def named(nodes, name):
    for node in nodes:
        if getattr(node, "name", None) == name:
            return node
        if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) and any(getattr(t, "id", None) == name for t in node.targets):
            return node
    raise LookupError(name)


def source(text, dotted):
    node, nodes = None, ast.parse(text).body
    for part in dotted.split("."):
        node = named(nodes, part)
        nodes = getattr(node, "body", [])
    return ast.get_source_segment(text, node)


def base_text(path):
    return subprocess.run(["git", "show", f"{BASE}:{path}"], capture_output=True, text=True,
                          check=True).stdout


for path, names in LISTED.items():
    base, tip = base_text(path), open(path, encoding="utf-8").read()
    for name in names:
        was, now = source(base, name), source(tip, name)
        print("same" if was == now else "DIFFERS", path, name)
        if was != now:
            sys.stdout.writelines(difflib.unified_diff(
                was.splitlines(keepends=True), now.splitlines(keepends=True),
                f"{BASE}:{path}:{name}", f"tip:{path}:{name}"))
base, tip = base_text("wl_xcon/taskd.py"), open("wl_xcon/taskd.py", encoding="utf-8").read()
command, was = source(tip, "Session._command"), source(base, "Session._command")
both = C2A + C2B
print("added once" if command.count(both) == 1 else "ADDED NOT FOUND ONCE", "Session._command")
print("same" if command.replace(both, "") == was.replace(C2A, "") else "DIFFERS",
      "wl_xcon/taskd.py Session._command, its five added branches taken out")
EOF
git diff --stat "$BASE" -- wl_xcon/bounds.py wl_xcon/welfare.py wl_xcon/marks.py wl_xcon/stranded.py
git show "$BASE":wl_xcon/cli.py | grep -c '_marks.depart(session, departure)'; grep -c '_marks.depart(session, departure)' wl_xcon/cli.py
```

If anything else differs, it goes to the PI in plain terms before the merge.

So it printed in the dry run, the `_start` diff exactly those three lines, and nothing else differing (the run's output kept in the plan's workspace, `.superpowers/c2b-plan/work4/welfare_out.txt`).

- [ ] **Step 4: The sweep.** As C2a did: **CI's per-push gate** sweeps every function of each module a push changes (`--changed-only --shard k/12`); after the last task, one CI run of the branch against `main` is read **shard by shard**, every line a real `N failed`, never the job's color, and a shard's log counts as read only if it ends with the gate's closing line and "Cleaning up orphan processes" (`gh run view --log` drops lines past a 64 KiB one; the per-job API, `gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/actions/jobs/<id>/logs`, has them all). The modules: `task`, `scheduler`, `check`, `taskd`, `record`, `simulate`, `link`, `serve`, `service`, `preflight`, `cli`, `encode`, `web`, `review` -- none new, so `tools/mutation_gate.py`'s lists stay as they are. Tasks 4 and 8 change `tests/_sessions.py` and `tests/_frames.py`, which are in `mutation_gate.GLOBAL`, so those pushes' gates escalate to a full sweep anyway. Each function caught only by `timed out after 300s` or an import error (XC-275's verdicts) is then neutered locally (`tools/mutate.py`'s own `_neuter_source`) with its own test file run alone, and must fail a named test, against a control of the same files unmutated. A survivor gets a test in a fix commit before anything else; one that cannot get a test is filed.

The Status table's load-time checks row gains `bad-plan` (a refusal) and the nine plan warnings.

## Not in C2b

What later builds carry, so no reader expects it here:

- **C3, varying conditions and the live-edit layer**: drawn values and the refusal of a parameter set by a condition and drawn both (engine spec §14.1; scope R7, which has nothing to bite on until drawn values exist); the console showing which conditions a live edit overrides (§14.7), where C2b records it on each trial's row and names them on the edit's feed line; the change log; **the operator's edits at the rig**, conditions and block types added or removed and checked as the task file is (§13.2; decision 38) -- and with them a plan made at the rig beyond the task's own (XC-207's "or the rig picks one", call 25); the designed orders (priming, sequence-balanced) and their repeat rule (XC-361).
- **C5, the condition registry**: condition numbers fixed across task versions, and `CONDITION` (0x8003) strobed in each trial (the PI's answer Q11; XC-197, re-pointed in Task 7). Until then a trial's condition is its name in `trials.jsonl`.
- **C6, programs, rig edits and interludes**: interludes (§13.4: the run steps aside and returns to the same block; an interlude's trials belong to no block, so wl-preproc would need a `BLOCK_END` before it and a new `BLOCK_START` after, neighbors §2.2), session programs (§13.7), and what a block declares to do on resuming after an interruption (TSK-16; scope §7 risk 11).
- **C8**: the review report's census per condition and block, a plan's structures among them, and its timing diagram (§18.1); the simulator playing a plan (call 30).
- **D and C7, procedures and stages**: an animal's stage carried from session to session (XC-288's stages, which a plan is not -- Task 1's word to wl-xtasks), and so **a training progression started at the stage an animal reached** (XC-405, filed in Task 5); until then a trainer moves it up with *next block*.
- **Block orders the PI was offered and did not choose** (Q8's B and C): mirror order (ABBA) and equal transitions (de Bruijn-like) between blocks [@brooks2012counterbalancing; @aguirre2011de]; either can be added as an order of its own.
- **`wlx console` showing schema 16's and 17's additions** (XC-387, extended in Task 8): waits on a decision that the terminal console is kept.
- **XC-206**: `BLOCK_END` at an advance still goes out at the boundary that advances (call 23).
- **The trial clock measured on a rig** (XC-406, filed in Task 7): until then a time limit is nominal.
- **wl-preproc reading C2b's fields** (XC-404; the ask written in Task 1, sent before the merge).
- **Positions on the console, eccentricity and direction first** (XC-401): the page and `wlx console` show a setting's value as they do today; a setting holds a `Polar` in the run's own values only when code puts it there, a console sending numbers and words alone, so the console never meets one from a plan (Task 2).

## Appendix A: Welfare summary items

**The welfare-critical list is not widened, and no limit is softened.** Task 12 compares every listed function, module and line with the base, byte for byte: `bounds.py`, `welfare.py`, `marks.py` and `stranded.py` whole; `preflight.gate` and `out_of_cage`; `Service._open`, `_end`, `_unended`, `_close_stranded`, `_unasked`, `_folder_name`; `Session._ends`, `_hold`, `_manual_reward`, `set`, `_schedule` and `OUTSIDE_A_RUN`; `link._setting`; `cli._settle_departure`, `_settle_return` and `main`'s `_marks.depart(session, departure)` line -- each the same; `Service._start`, its three lines below; and `Session._command` with its five added branches taken out.

**Approved by the PI on 2026-10-19**: items 1 and 2, "Approve" each. In plain words, as he approved them:

1. **The function that starts a run (`Service._start`, on the list) changes in three lines -- approved, 2026-10-19.** It now hands the run the plan you chose (one line), it takes the run's pre-flight for that plan (one line), and its description, which said "until [plans] exist a run is one block", now says the run plays the plan its start names, or the plain run. A plan the task does not have fails the pre-flight's task checks, which the existing gate refuses, so the function gains no new decision of its own. Everything else in it -- the out-of-cage check, the gate, the acknowledgments, writing nothing itself -- is unchanged. Task 12 prints the three-line difference.
2. **Two new console buttons pass through the console's command handler (`Session._command`, part of which is on the list) -- approved, 2026-10-19.** *Next block* and *back one* (your answer Q10) are two new branches beside the pause, as your three C2a buttons are, each handing to a function off the list (`Session._move`). **The listed parts do not change**: the line that hands every manual reward to its checker, and the one line that turns a malformed setting into a refusal. Neither button touches a reward, a reward size, a clock or the pump. Like the pause, one pressed while paused is applied while paused. A button press acts at once: a drummed trial waiting to come back is dropped, and the record says so.
3. **What C2a built reaches an animal for the first time, through a plan -- for your information.** Until now every live run was the plain "run N trials" run, which counts every trial once and shows nothing again; your C2a approval said of the rest, "No live run has such a block before C2b". With a plan, each task's own counting tables apply: **a break can be shown again; a condition with no repeat limit is repeated until done; a drummed trial comes back until it is right; and a block that ends on a criterion runs past its targets until the criterion is met.** None of this changes how a trial is paid -- a correct trial pays the same `reward_correct`, through the same path -- and **the out-of-cage limit still ends the session whatever a block still owes**, checked between trials and while paused, as before. A trainer can still *clear* a trial, stop drumming, move on a block by hand, or stop the run.
4. **A plan's time limits are not welfare limits, and do not use the welfare stop's name -- for your information.** A block's or a run's time limit counts the time trials run (a pause does not count, your answer Q6) and is read between trials, so a trial in flight finishes and pays (Q1). **And a drummed trial still coming back is finished first, for a block's limit and a run's alike** (your answers "Finish the drum first"). So a time limit can run over by more than one trial: by as long as that drummed trial takes to be answered right. Nothing else bounds that wait but its being right, the trainer switching drumming off or moving on a block, a person stopping the run -- the console warns after ten failed tries in a row -- or the out-of-cage limit, which ends the session whatever a plan says. A run its time limit ends is recorded and shown as completed, with its own sentence -- never as `limit`, the name the record, the page and wl-works give the out-of-cage stop. Neither limit is read inside the welfare-listed code that checks the out-of-cage limit or holds a pause; nothing acts during a pause but what acted before.
5. **A block can switch drumming on from a run's first trial -- for your information.** A plan's block that declares drumming (built in C2a) is how drumming is on from the start, where until now a trainer switched it on after starting. The standing banner, and its warning outside a training session, apply as before.
6. **Plans can make runs long -- for your information.** A run goes on until a person stops it, a scheduled stop or the out-of-cage limit ends it when its plan repeats its blocks with no count, or when it is a training progression -- unless the plan has a run time limit, or every stage has a time limit and no fall-back rule moves the animal back (an animal that never passes an untimed stage works on there; a fall-back can move it up and back between timed stages without end). The load-time check warns of exactly these (`endless-plan`), saying so. A block that ends only on a criterion can also go on for as long as the animal does not meet it, as in C2a.
7. **Reward is untouched -- for your information.** A condition sets the task's own settings only: one that names a setting the task does not declare -- a reward size among them -- is refused at load. Reward still goes by the bounded config's named entry, never an amount.

## Appendix B: For the PI to confirm

What your sixteen answers of 2026-10-19 leave open, each built one way and cheap to change. **The PI confirmed all as built, 2026-10-19** ("Confirm all as built").

1. **A live edit beats the condition's value for the rest of the run** (the spec, §14.7; the old code did the reverse). The trial still counts toward its condition and its record names the value you overrode. Two things to know: the console does not yet show which conditions an edit overrides (build C3) -- until then the edit's line on the changes feed names them -- and setting the value back does not hand control back to the condition; it stays yours until the run ends.
2. **At a training plan's top stage, its own time limit ends the run** (your Q3 and Q4 together: the top stage keeps going, a timed stage moves on, and there is nowhere to move on to).
3. **"Time" means the time trials ran**: each trial's frames and its inter-trial gap, added up as each trial ends; neither a pause nor the console's housekeeping between trials counts. The figure is nominal: it assumes every frame took its nominal length, so on a rig that drops frames a limit means somewhat more wall time than it says. Measuring that on a rig is filed (XC-406).
4. **A block with a number of trials still shows every condition its target**, as a criterion block does (your ruling of 2026-10-18); past the targets every condition is drawn by its order until the number is run.
5. **The first stage of a training plan has no stage below it, so its fall-back rule does nothing there**; a plan of one stage with a fall-back rule gets a load warning saying so.
6. **One fall-back rule per plan, for every stage** ("a second rule the plan states").
7. **When a stage's rule and its fall-back are met at the same answer, the animal moves up** ("training ends on a success"); at the top stage, the fall-back waits for as long as the stage's rule holds. A plan whose fall-back bar sits at or above a stage's pass bar, or whose stage judges fewer answers than the fall-back does, gets a load warning that the animal may move up and straight back.
8. **When a timed stage's fall-back is met at the same moment its time runs out, the animal moves back** (not on, as "Move on anyway" would read alone): the fall-back says the animal is struggling there. Rare now that the fall-back reads first answers only. The alternative, moving on, is one line.
9. **A person's *back one* replays the block before the open one, then the plan carries on** -- so the block left comes again; in a training plan it is the stage below. A button press acts at once, dropping a drummed trial waiting (no "finish the drum first" for a person's move); the record names the dropped trial.
10. **A plan that cannot go on ends its run as completed**, with its sentence saying why, and the record says which of four reasons (plan done, plan time, stalled, cannot draw).
11. **"Never back-to-back" with only two block types makes a balanced plan alternate** after its first round (A B A B ..., the seed choosing which comes first); with three or more, each round is still shuffled.
12. **On the page, the plain run reads "plain run: the trials below, each counted once, none shown again"**, and the trials box is hidden while a plan is chosen.
13. **No reference task gets a plan**: the plans in the tests are engine test material, written by the tests themselves; real plans are yours, set in wl-xtasks.
